II. Software Requirement Specification
1. Overall Requirements
1.1 Context Diagram
The Duy Long Computer Warehouse Management System is developed to support warehouse operations and warranty management activities at Duy Long Computer. The system provides functionalities for inventory management, product management, stock import and export, warranty processing, user management, and reporting.
The context diagram illustrates the system boundary and its interactions with external entities, including Super Admin, Manager, Staff, and Mail Service. These entities exchange information with the system to perform daily operational activities such as managing products, inventory transactions, warranty requests, user accounts, reports, and notifications.
The context diagram provides a high-level overview of the system and defines the external interfaces involved in warehouse and warranty management processes.

The link of the feature tree: Context Diagram

1.3 User Requirements
1.3.1 Actors
#	Actor	Description
1	Super Admin	Responsible for managing user accounts, role permissions, system configurations, and monitoring system activities through audit logs and reports.
2	Manager	Responsible for managing products, inventory, stock imports, warranty operations, and monitoring business performance through reports and dashboards.
3	Staff	Responsible for performing daily operational activities, including inventory transactions, sales order processing, and warranty handling.
4	Mail Service	External service used to send OTP verification codes and system notifications to users.

1.4.3 Non-UI Functions
#	Feature	System Function	Description
1	Authentication & Authorization	JWT Authentication Service	Generate, validate, and refresh JWT tokens for user authentication and secure API access.
2	Authentication & Authorization	Permission Validation Service	Verify user permissions and roles before allowing access to protected functions and APIs.
3	Customer Management	OTP Verification Service	Generate and validate OTP codes for customer identity verification during registration and online order creation.
4	Customer Management	Customer Lookup API	Retrieve customer information using phone number or email for sales and warranty processes.
5	Inventory Management	Inventory Quantity Update Service	Automatically update inventory quantities after import, export, transfer, assembly, and warranty transactions.
6	Inventory Management	Inventory Validation Service	Validate stock availability and prevent invalid inventory transactions.
7	Inventory Management	Inventory Transfer Service	Process inventory movement between warehouses and update stock records accordingly.
8	Product Management	Barcode/QR Scanning Service	Decode barcode and QR code data to identify products and automatically populate product information.
9	Product Management	BOM Processing Service	Consume component inventory and create finished products based on predefined BOM configurations.
10	Order Management	Online Order Synchronization Service	Synchronize online customer orders with POS and warehouse export processes.
11	Import Management	Import Slip Number Generator	Automatically generate unique import slip identifiers.
12	Export Management	Export Slip Number Generator	Automatically generate unique export slip identifiers.
13	Warranty Management	Warranty Status Tracking Service	Track warranty progress and update repair or warranty request statuses.
14	Warranty Management	Warranty Expiration Validation Service	Verify whether purchased products are still covered under warranty policies.
15	Notification	Email Notification Service	Send emails for account activation, warranty updates, order confirmations, and password recovery.
16	Notification	Warranty Notification Service	Notify customers when warranty status changes or products are ready for collection.
17	Audit & Logging	Audit Logging Service	Record user activities such as login, account changes, permission updates, and inventory transactions.
18	Reporting	Inventory Report Generator	Generate inventory reports including stock balance, import/export history, and inventory movement.
19	Reporting	Debt Report Generator	Generate customer and supplier debt reports and payment summaries.
20	Security	Password Encryption Service	Encrypt and securely store user passwords using hashing algorithms.
21	Security	Route Protection Middleware	Prevent unauthorized direct access to protected APIs and system resources.
22	Data Management	Data Backup Service	Perform scheduled backup of system data for recovery purposes.
23	Data Integration	MISA Data Import Service	Import historical product, customer, supplier, and transaction data exported from MISA.
24	System Monitoring	System Audit Monitoring Service	Monitor and maintain system audit logs for security and compliance purposes.
25	Notification	Unprocessed Order Alert Service	Trigger alerts when customer orders remain unprocessed beyond a configured period (e.g., 5 minutes).

Table 04: Non-UI Function List
1.5 Entity Relationship Diagram
      
Figure 10: ERD Conceptual Diagram
The link of the diagram: ERD Conceptual Diagram
Entities Description
#	Entity	Description
1	Partner	Represents external entities such as Customers and Suppliers. A partner supplies Purchase Orders, places Sale Orders, and has associated Payments.
2	User	Represents internal system employees/staff. Users interact with the system to create key operational documents, including Payments, Sale Orders, Purchase Orders, and Inventory Documents.
3	Payment	Represents financial transactions (inflow/outflow). Created by a User and linked to a specific Partner.
4	Purchase Order	Represents procurement orders supplied by a Partner. It contains a list of Products and, upon execution, automatically generates Inventory Documents (Import Slips).
5	Sale Order	Represents customer orders placed by a Partner. It contains a list of Products and, upon execution, automatically generates Inventory Documents (Export Slips).
6	Assembly Order	Represents manufacturing, assembly, or disassembly tasks. It contains Products (components/finished goods) and generates Inventory Documents to handle component consumption and product receiving.
7	Inventory Document	Represents warehouse stock movements (e.g., Import, Export, or Transfer slips). It can be generated automatically by Orders (PO, SO, Assembly) or created manually by a User, and it directly writes data to update the Inventory Balance.
8	Inventory Balance	Represents the detailed on-hand stock ledger. It tracks the real-time quantity of each Product and indicates the specific Warehouse where the stock is stored.
9	Warehouse	Represents the physical storage locations. It stores the Inventory Balances of various products.
10	Product	The core master data representing goods or items. Products are contained in Orders (PO, SO, Assembly), tracked by Inventory Balances, and individually managed through Serial Numbers.
11	Serial Number	A unique identifier for a specific physical unit of a Product, enabling deep traceability and after-sales services.
12	Warranty	Represents the warranty policies, coverage, and expiration timelines associated with a specific Serial Number.
13	Repair	Represents a maintenance request or repair ticket. It records service actions linked to and covered by an existing Warranty.

Table 05: Entity Description

2. Use Case Specifications
2.1 Authentication
2.1.1 UC 01 - Login
Primary Actors	User (Staff, Manager, Admin)	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a User (Staff, Manager, Admin), I want to login to the system using my registered username and password so that I can be authenticated and access my authorized features such as inventory management, sales, or system administration based on my role.
Preconditions	- The user must have a valid account registered and existing in the system's database.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally.
Postconditions	- The user's session is initialized (Receives an authenticated JWT Token).
- The system grants permissions based on the account's Role and redirects the user to the corresponding homepage/dashboard.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user accesses the application and navigates to the Login screen.
2. The system displays a login form requiring: Username and Password.
3. The user enters a valid Username and Password, then clicks the "Login" button.
4. The system (Frontend) sends an authentication request containing the credentials to the Backend.
5. The Backend verifies the username and password against the database records.
6. Upon successful verification, the Backend generates a JWT Token (containing user ID, username, and Role) and returns it to the Frontend.
7. The system (Frontend) saves the JWT Token and redirects the user to the main interface corresponding to their permissions.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Anticipated error conditions:

E1. Missing mandatory information:
- At step 3 of the Normal Flow, if the user leaves the Username or Password field blank.
- The system prevents submission and displays a validation warning (e.g., "Username cannot be empty" or "Password cannot be empty").

E2. Incorrect login credentials:
- At step 5 of the Normal Flow, if the Username or Password does not match the database records.
- The Backend rejects the authentication.
- The system displays an error message "Incorrect login credentials" and prompts the user to try again.

2.1.2 UC 02 - Login with Google
Primary Actors
User (Staff, Manager, Admin)	Secondary Actors	Google Authentication System (Google OAuth2)
Description	As a User (Staff, Manager, Admin), I want to login to the system using my Google account so that I can quickly authenticate without remembering a separate password and access my authorized features based on my role.
Preconditions	User account has been created & authorized
Postconditions	●	User logs in the system successfully
●	The system tracked successful login into the Activity Log
Normal Sequence/Flow	Login System
1.	User clicks Login button from the page header or accesses an authenticated feature (from a link or type the page URL directly into the address bar)
2.	System show the User Login screen
3.	User types in the login details (email, password)
4.	User clicks the Login button
5.	System validates the login details (BR-01, BR-02)
6.	System allows user to access
7.	System tracks user’s success login to the Activity Log
8.	System directs user to the Home Page (or the previous calling page if any)
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1_Google Login
1.	User clicks Google Login button to login system using Google account
2.	System redirects the user to the Google’s Login screen
3.	User types in the Google account details and chooses to login
4.	Google validates user’s login information successfully and redirect him/her back to the system
5.	Return to step 5 of normal flow.
Step 4_System can’t authenticate the user
User can’t be authenticated & get relevant error message in one of below cases
1.	He/she leaves the Email and/or Password field blank (MSG10)
2.	Input Email or Password are incorrect (MSG09)
3.	Input Email/Password are correct but email has not been verified (MSG11)
4.	The user account is blocked / inactive (MSG12)
If user inputs wrong logging-in details 5 times continuously, system will lock his/her account in 30 minutes (with relevant warning message - MSG13)

2.1.3 UC 03 - Logout

Primary Actors	Super Admin, Manager, Staff (Authenticated Users)	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As an authenticated user (Super Admin, Manager, or Staff), I want to log out of the system so that my active session is terminated, preventing unauthorized access to the warehouse management system.
Preconditions	The user must be logged into the system and have an active session (valid token and role present in localStorage).
Postconditions	●	The active session is terminated (all credentials, tokens, and roles are cleared from localStorage).
●	The user is redirected to the Login page (/login).
●	The user is prevented from accessing protected routes without re-authenticating.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks or hovers over the User Profile dropdown menu (with initials/avatar and username) at the top-right corner of the interface (header).
2.	The system displays the user dropdown menu containing the option Đăng xuất.
3.	The user clicks the Đăng xuất option.
4.	The system clears all user session data from local storage (executes localStorage.clear()).
5.	The system redirects the user to the Login page (/login).
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Automatic Logout on Token Expiration/Invalidation (HTTP 401)
1.	The user performs an action that triggers a request to the backend API.
2.	The backend detects that the JWT token is expired, invalid, or the account has been disabled/blocked, and returns an HTTP 401 Unauthorized status.
3.	The frontend's axios response interceptor captures the 401 status code.
4.	The system automatically removes token and role from localStorage (via localStorage.removeItem('token') and localStorage.removeItem('role')).
5.	The system redirects the user to the Login page (/login).
Alternative Flow 2: Attempting to access protected routes post-logout
1.	The user attempts to manually access any protected page (e.g., /dashboard, /import-history) via the browser's address bar after logging out.
2.	The system's route guard (ProtectedRoute) checks localStorage and finds no token.
3.	The system redirects the user to the Login page (/login) and prevents access to the page content.

2.1.3 UC 04 - Change Password

Primary Actors	Authenticated User	Secondary Actors	Backend System
Description	As a logged-in user, I want to change my current password to a new one so that I can maintain the security of my account and prevent unauthorized access.
Preconditions	The user must be successfully logged into the system and navigating to the "Change Password" section.
Postconditions	The user's password is successfully updated in the database. The user can use the new password for future logins.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks on their profile avatar/dropdown in the header and selects the "Đổi mật khẩu" (Change Password) option.
2.	The system displays the Change Password page.
3.	The user enters their current password, their new password, and confirms the new password.
4.	The user clicks the "Cập nhật mật khẩu" (Update Password) button.
5.	The system validates the inputs, verifying that the current password matches the database record and that the new password and confirmation match.
6.	The system securely updates the password in the database.
7.	The system displays a success message indicating the password was successfully changed.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	●	5a. Incorrect Current Password: If the current password entered by the user is incorrect, the system displays an error message and stops the update process.
●	5b. Passwords Do Not Match: If the new password and confirmation password do not match, the system alerts the user and prevents form submission.
●	5c. Password Complexity Not Met: If the new password lacks required complexity (e.g., must have at least 8 characters including letters and numbers), the system displays a validation error message.
●	4a. User Cancels: If the user clicks the "Hủy bỏ" (Cancel) button at any point, the system discards any input and redirects the user back to the Dashboard page.


Primary Actors	Unauthenticated User	Secondary Actors	Backend System, Email Server
Description	As an unauthenticated user, I want to reset my password using my registered email address so that I can regain access to my account if I forget my password. This process involves verifying the user's identity via a One-Time Password (OTP) sent to their email.
Preconditions	The user must have a registered account with a valid email address. The user is currently not logged into the system.
Postconditions	The user's password is successfully updated in the system, and they are able to log in with the new password.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user navigates to the "Forgot Password" page from the Login screen.
2.	The system prompts the user to enter their registered email address.
3.	The user enters their email and submits the request.
4.	The system validates the email and sends an OTP to the provided email address. The UI transitions to the "Verify OTP" step.
5.	The user retrieves the OTP from their email inbox, enters it into the system, and submits.
6.	The system verifies the OTP. Upon successful verification, the UI transitions to the "Reset Password" step.
7.	The user enters a new password, confirms it, and submits.
8.	The system securely updates the password in the database and displays a success notification via a Toast message.
9.	The system automatically redirects the user back to the Login page after a short delay.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	4a. Email Not Found/Invalid: If the email format is invalid or does not exist in the database, the system displays an error message and prompts the user to try again.
6a. Invalid or Expired OTP: If the entered OTP is incorrect or has expired, the system shows an error message and requires the user to re-enter the OTP or request a new one.
8a. Password Requirements Not Met: If the new password does not meet the security criteria, the system rejects the request and displays a validation error message on the form.

2.1.4 UC 05 - Forgot Password

Primary Actors	Unauthenticated User	Secondary Actors	Backend System, Email Server
Description	As an unauthenticated user, I want to reset my password using my registered email address so that I can regain access to my account if I forget my password. This process involves verifying the user's identity via a One-Time Password (OTP) sent to their email.
Preconditions	The user must have a registered account with a valid email address. The user is currently not logged into the system.
Postconditions	The user's password is successfully updated in the system, and they are able to log in with the new password.
Normal Sequence/Flow	10.	The user navigates to the "Forgot Password" page from the Login screen.
11.	The system prompts the user to enter their registered email address.
12.	The user enters their email and submits the request.
13.	The system validates the email and sends an OTP to the provided email address. The UI transitions to the "Verify OTP" step.
14.	The user retrieves the OTP from their email inbox, enters it into the system, and submits.
15.	The system verifies the OTP. Upon successful verification, the UI transitions to the "Reset Password" step.
16.	The user enters a new password, confirms it, and submits.
17.	The system securely updates the password in the database and displays a success notification via a Toast message.
18.	The system automatically redirects the user back to the Login page after a short delay.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	4a. Email Not Found/Invalid: If the email format is invalid or does not exist in the database, the system displays an error message and prompts the user to try again.
6a. Invalid or Expired OTP: If the entered OTP is incorrect or has expired, the system shows an error message and requires the user to re-enter the OTP or request a new one.
8a. Password Requirements Not Met: If the new password does not meet the security criteria, the system rejects the request and displays a validation error message on the form.

2.2 Profile Management
2.2.1 UC 06 - View Profile
Primary Actors	Super Admin, Manager, Staff (Authenticated Users)	Secondary Actors	Database System (Mocked in Frontend)
Description	As an authenticated user (Super Admin, Manager, or Staff), I want to view my profile details (Full name, Email, Phone number, and System role) so that I can verify my identity information and permissions in the warehouse management system.
Preconditions	The user must be logged into the system and have an active session (valid token and role present in localStorage).
Postconditions	The system successfully displays the user's profile page containing the user's full name, email, phone number, and system role.
The user session remains active.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks or hovers over the User Profile dropdown menu (showing avatar/initials) at the top-right corner of the interface (header).
2.	The system displays the user dropdown menu containing the option Xem thông tin cá nhân.
3.	The user clicks the Xem thông tin cá nhân option.
4.	The system navigates the user to the Profile page (/profile).
5.	The system renders the user's profile card containing:Họ và Tên (Full Name)
6.	Email
7.	Số điện thoại (Phone number)
8.	Vai trò hệ thống (System role)
9.	Note: While the backend has a GET /api/v1/users/me API implemented, the frontend currently renders static/mocked profile details based on the user's role in localStorage instead of calling the backend API.
10.	The user is presented with two action options:Click Đổi mật khẩu to navigate to the change password page (/change-password).
11.	Click Quay lại Dashboard to navigate back to the dashboard page (/dashboard).
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: 
1.	Session Expiration on navigationThe user attempts to navigate to the profile page, but the local token has expired.
2.	The system redirects the user to the Login page (/login) via route guards.

2.2.2 UC 07 - Update Profile
Primary Actors	Super Admin, Manager, Staff (Authenticated Users)	Secondary Actors	Database System
Description	As an authenticated user (Super Admin, Manager, or Staff), I want to update my profile details (Full name, Phone number, Address, or Avatar) so that my contact information remains up to date in the system.
Preconditions	The user must be logged into the system and have an active session (valid token and role present in localStorage). The user must be on their Profile page (/profile).
Postconditions	The user's profile information is successfully updated in the database.
The updated information is rendered on the Profile page.
An audit log is recorded on the backend to log the user profile update event.
Normal Sequence/Flow	The user navigates to the Profile page (/profile) and clicks the proposed Chỉnh sửa thông tin (Edit Info) button.
The system displays the profile details in editable input fields (Họ và Tên, Số điện thoại, Địa chỉ). System role remains read-only.
The user modifies the desired fields.
The user clicks the proposed Lưu thay đổi (Save Changes) button.
The system validates the inputs (e.g., non-empty name, valid phone number format).
The system sends an update request (proposed PUT /api/v1/users/me) containing the updated information to the backend.
The backend updates the database, records an audit log event, and returns an HTTP 200 OK status.
The frontend displays a success message "Cập nhật hồ sơ thành công" and switches back to read-only mode displaying the new data.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: 
1.	User cancels the editThe user clicks the proposed Hủy (Cancel) button while in edit mode.
2.	The system discards any unsaved modifications and reverts the profile page to read-only mode.
Alternative Flow 2: Validation errors (e.g., Invalid Phone Number Format)
1.	The user enters an invalid phone number or leaves required fields empty and clicks Lưu thay đổi.
2.	The system prevents submission and displays an inline validation error message (e.g., "Số điện thoại không hợp lệ" or "Họ và tên không được để trống").
Alternative Flow 3: Backend Database / Network Error
1.	The server is unreachable or fails to save changes, returning an HTTP 500 error.
2.	The system displays an error message "Lỗi kết nối hệ thống. Vui lòng thử lại sau" and keeps the user in edit mode with their input preserved.
2.3 User Management
2.2.1 UC 08 - View Account List

Primary Actors	Super Admin, Manage	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Super Admin or Manager, I want to view the list of user accounts so that I can monitor system personnel, manage their access, review their statuses, and view summary statistics.
Preconditions	1. The actor must be successfully authenticated and logged into the system.
2. The actor must hold the necessary authorization role (SUPER_ADMIN or MANAGER).
Postconditions	The system successfully retrieves and displays the list of user accounts along with system-wide account statistics (Total staff, Active, Pending, Inactive).
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor navigates to the "Quản lý người dùng" page from the navigation menu.
2. The system verifies the actor's permissions.
3. The system fetches the list of all user accounts and available roles from the database via the API.
4. The system calculates real-time account statistics and displays them in cards: TỔNG NHÂN SỰ, ĐANG LÀM VIỆC, CHỜ PHÊ DUYỆT, and KHÓA TÀI KHOẢN.
5. The system displays the account list in a tabular format with the exact columns: AVATAR, HỌ VÀ TÊN, MÃ NHÂN VIÊN, BỘ PHẬN, VAI TRÒ, EMAIL, TRẠNG THÁI, and THAO TÁC.
6. The system displays pagination controls at the bottom of the table.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Other successful usage scenarios:

1. Search and Filter Accounts:
- 1.1. In step 5 of the normal flow, the actor enters a keyword in the search bar or clicks the "Bộ lọc" button to apply filter criteria.
- 1.2. The system filters the account list based on the input and immediately updates the table to display the matching results.

2. View Account Details:
- 2.1. In step 5 of the normal flow, the actor clicks on a specific user row or clicks the three-dots icon in the THAO TÁC column and selects "Xem thông tin chi tiết".
- 2.2. The system opens the Employee Drawer displaying comprehensive information about the selected user.

Anticipated error conditions:

1. No Users Found:
- If the database has no users or no users match the applied search/filter criteria, the system displays the message "Không có người dùng nào." inside the table instead of the data rows.

2. Data Fetch Failure:
- If the system fails to retrieve the user list due to a server or network error, the system logs the error to the console and may display an empty table or a loading error state.

3. Unauthorized Access:
- If an actor without the required roles attempts to access the list, the backend API will reject the request (403 Forbidden) and the system will deny access.

2.2.2 UC 09 - Create Account
Primary Actors	Super Admin (or Administrator)	Secondary Actors	Database System
Description	As a Super Admin, I want to create a new employee account in the system so that they can access the warehouse management system with appropriate system roles.
Preconditions	The Super Admin is logged in and has an active session with the account:add permission.
Postconditions	A new user is successfully saved in the database with the default status APPROVED, a hashed default password (123456), and assigned roles.
Normal Sequence/Flow	The Admin navigates to the User Management page (/users) and clicks the Thêm thành viên button.
1.	The system redirects the Admin to the create user page (/users/create).
2.	The Admin fills out the employee form containing:Họ và tên (Required)
3.	Tên đăng nhập (Username) (Required)
4.	Số điện thoại
5.	Địa chỉ Email
6.	Ngày sinh, Ngày vào làm việc chính thức
7.	Số CCCD (Required)
8.	Giới tính (Male / Female / Other)
9.	Chức danh nhân sự, Phòng ban
10.	Địa chỉ thường trú

The Admin tick or unticks the checkbox card Thiết lập tài khoản quản lý hệ thống (Super Admin) to decide the user role.
1.	The Admin clicks the Lưu người dùng button in the action bar.
2.	The system validates the required fields (Họ và tên, Tên đăng nhập).
3.	The system sends a POST request to /api/v1/users with the payload (username, fullName, email, phone, status: 'APPROVED', and roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] or ['STAFF']).
4.	The backend hashes the default password, saves the user to the database, logs a CREATE event in the audit log, and returns success (HTTP 200 OK).
5.	The system redirects the Admin back to the User Management list page (/users).
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Missing required fields
1.	The Admin leaves Họ và tên or Tên đăng nhập (Username) blank and clicks Lưu người dùng.
2.	The browser shows an alert: "Vui lòng nhập Họ và tên" or "Vui lòng nhập Tên đăng nhập (Username)".
3.	The system halts submission.
Alternative Flow 2: Username already exists / server-side error
1.	The server returns an error code stating the username is already taken.
2.	The system shows an alert displaying the error message from the API.
Alternative Flow 3: Cancel Account Creation
1.	The Admin clicks Hủy bỏ.
2.	The system discards any input and redirects the Admin back to /users.

2.2.3 UC 10 View Account Details
Primary Actors	Customer	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Super Admin or Manager, I want to view the detailed information of a specific user account so that I can verify their contact details, organizational role, personal information, and assigned system permissions.
Preconditions	1. The actor must be successfully authenticated and logged into the system.
2. The actor must hold the necessary authorization role (SUPER_ADMIN or MANAGER).
3. The actor must be on the "Quản lý người dùng" (User Management) page and the account list must be loaded.
Postconditions	The system successfully opens the Employee Drawer and displays the complete, categorized details of the selected user without modifying any data.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor selects a user by clicking directly on their row in the table, OR clicks the three-dots icon in the THAO TÁC column and selects "Xem thông tin chi tiết".
2. The system retrieves the selected user's detailed profile information.
3. The system opens the Employee Drawer on the side of the screen.
4. The system displays the user's header profile, which includes: Avatar, Full Name, Employee Code (Mã NV), Status, and Role badge.
5. The system defaults to displaying the "Thông tin chung" tab, showing: Email công việc, Số điện thoại, Bộ phận, and Chức danh.
6. The actor clicks the "Thông tin nhân viên" tab. The system switches the view to show personal and contract details: Ngày sinh, Giới tính, Địa chỉ thường trú, Số CMND/CCCD, Ngày chính thức, Loại hợp đồng, and Trạng thái.
7. The actor clicks the "Chức năng/Vai trò" tab. The system displays the user's system role access level (either Quản lý hệ thống or Người sử dụng hệ thống).
8. The actor clicks the close icon (X) at the top right of the drawer.
9. The system closes the drawer and returns the actor to the main account list view.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Other successful usage scenarios:
1. Switch to Edit Mode:
- 1.1. In steps 5, 6, or 7 of the normal flow, the actor decides to modify the user's information and clicks the "Chỉnh sửa" button at the bottom of the drawer.
- 1.2. The system transitions the view text into editable form inputs (Input fields, Dropdown selects, Radio buttons) and displays the "Hủy" and "Lưu thay đổi" buttons.

Anticipated error conditions:
1. Data Retrieval Failure:
- If the system fails to fetch the complete user details from the database due to network issues or deleted data, the system will log the error and either display an empty drawer or show an error alert notifying the actor that the data cannot be loaded.

2.2.4 UC 11 Update Information
Primary Actors	Super Admin (or Administrator)	Secondary Actors	Database System
Description	As a Super Admin, I want to edit an employee's profile information and roles from the employee drawer so that the employee data remains accurate.
Preconditions	The Super Admin is logged in and has an active session with the account:edit permission.
Postconditions	The employee's details (Full name, email, phone, and roles) are successfully updated in the database and logged.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The Admin navigates to the User Management page (/users) and clicks on a user row.
2.	The system slides open the EmployeeDrawer in read-only mode, showing tabs: Thông tin chung, Thông tin nhân viên, and Chức năng/Vai trò.
3.	The Admin clicks the Chỉnh sửa button in the footer of the drawer.
4.	The system displays editable inputs/dropdowns for fields (Email, Số điện thoại, Bộ phận, Chức danh, etc.).
5.	The Admin updates the fields and clicks Lưu thay đổi.
6.	The system displays a confirmation modal:
●	If system role is Admin, modal displays: "Xác nhận cấp quyền Quản trị tối cao". Admin clicks Xác nhận cấp quyền.
●	If system role is User, modal displays: "Xác nhận chuyển sang thiết lập quyền chi tiết". Admin clicks Tiếp tục.
7.	The frontend sends a PUT request to /api/v1/users/{id} with the updated info (fullName, email, phone, and roles).
8.	The backend updates the database, writes an UPDATE event in the audit log, and returns success (HTTP 200 OK).
9.	The frontend refreshes the users list. If Tiếp tục was clicked, the Admin is redirected to the permissions page (/users/{id}/permissions). If not, the drawer is closed.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Cancel changes inside drawer
1.	The Admin clicks Hủy in the drawer footer while in edit mode.
2.	The drawer reverts the inputs to original data and returns to read-only view.
Alternative Flow 2: Cancel from Confirmation Modal
1.	The Admin clicks Hủy inside the confirmation modal.
2.	The system closes the modal, leaving the drawer in edit mode with inputs preserved.

2.2.5 UC 12  Lock/ Unlock Account

Primary Actors	Super Admin	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Super Admin, I want to lock or unlock a user account so that I can restrict or restore their access to the system based on security concerns or their current employment status.
Preconditions	1. The actor must be successfully authenticated and logged into the system.
2. The actor must hold the SUPER_ADMIN authorization role.
3. The actor must be on the "Quản lý người dùng" page and the account list must be loaded.
Postconditions	The system successfully updates the selected user's status in the database (to INACTIVE or APPROVED), and refreshes the data table to reflect the new status badge.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor locates an active user in the table (Status is showing as "Đang hoạt động").
2. The actor clicks the three-dots icon in the THAO TÁC column for that specific user.
3. The system displays a dropdown action menu.
4. The actor clicks the "Khóa tài khoản" option.
5. The system sends an API request to the backend to change the user's status to INACTIVE.
6. The system successfully processes the update in the database.
7. The system automatically fetches the latest user list and updates the UI, changing the user's status badge to "Đã khóa".
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Other successful usage scenarios:
1. Unlock Account (Mở khóa tài khoản):
- 1.1. The actor locates a locked user in the table (Status is showing as "Đã khóa").
- 1.2. The actor clicks the three-dots icon in the THAO TÁC column.
- 1.3. The actor clicks the "Mở khóa tài khoản" option.
- 1.4. The system sends an API request to change the status to APPROVED.
- 1.5. The system successfully updates the database and refreshes the table, changing the user's status badge back to "Đang hoạt động".

Anticipated error conditions:
1. Status Update Failure:
- If the system encounters an error while updating the status (e.g., database connection lost, network timeout), the system logs the error to the console and displays a browser alert: "Có lỗi xảy ra khi thay đổi trạng thái tài khoản."
- The user's status on the UI remains unchanged.

2. Unauthorized Access:
- If an actor without the SUPER_ADMIN role (e.g., a Manager) attempts to perform this action via the API, the backend will return a 403 Forbidden error.
- The system will block the status change and display the standard error alert.

2.2.6 UC 13 - Update Functional Permissions 
Primary Actors	Super Admin (or Administrator)	Secondary Actors	Database System
Description	As a Super Admin, I want to dynamically assign/modify detailed module-level permissions for a Staff user so that their access is limited to their exact job requirements.
Preconditions	The Super Admin is logged in and has an active session with the auth:edit permission. The target account must have the STAFF role.
Postconditions	The updated permissions are saved in the database, immediately updating the Staff member's dynamic authorization rules.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The Admin clicks the action menu button (⋮) in the user list and selects Phân quyền chức năng (or gets redirected here from the Employee Drawer).
2.	The system redirects the Admin to the detailed permissions page (/users/{id}/permissions).
3.	The system checks if the user role includes STAFF, then displays the permission categories sidebar (Quản lý kho, Danh mục, Báo cáo, Quản trị hệ thống) and actions matrix (Toàn quyền, Xem, Thêm, Sửa, Xóa, Xuất Excel, In).
4.	The Admin selects a category and checks/unchecks the desired actions.
5.	The Admin clicks the Lưu thay đổi button in the footer.
6.	The system compiles checked permission codes (e.g. import:view) and sends a PUT request to /api/v1/users/{id}/permissions.
7.	The backend saves the list of permission codes to the database, logs the event in the audit log, and returns success (HTTP 200 OK).
8.	The system alerts "Cập nhật phân quyền thành công!" and redirects the Admin back to the User Management page (/users).
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: User is not STAFF (Dynamic permissions restricted)
1.	The system detects the target user is not a STAFF member.
2.	The system alerts: "Chỉ tài khoản Nhân viên (STAFF) mới được phép phân quyền động."
3.	The system redirects the Admin back to /users.
Alternative Flow 2: Cancel modifications
1.	The Admin clicks the Hủy button in the footer.
2.	The system discards all checkbox selections and redirects the Admin back to /users.

Postconditions

2. The system closes the modal without deleting the warehouse.

2.5.3 UC 22 - Create Supplier
Primary Actors
Manager
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Manager, I want to add a new supplier (Organization or Individual) into the system to manage purchases and payables.
Preconditions
The Manager must be logged into the system.
Postconditions
A new supplier record is created and appears in the supplier list.
Normal Sequence/Flow
1. From the Supplier list page, the Manager clicks the "Add new" (Thêm mới) button.
2. The system opens the "Add Supplier" modal, prompting the user to select the type (Organization or Individual).
3. The Manager fills in basic required and optional fields (Code, Tax Code, Phone, Supplier Name, Website, Address, Supplier Group).
4. The Manager switches between tabs (Contact Info, Payment Terms, Bank Accounts, Notes) to enter additional details.
5. The Manager clicks the "Save" (Lưu) button.
6. The system validates the input, creates the record, closes the modal, and shows a success notification.
Alternative Sequences/Flows
Alternative Flow 1: Save & Add Another
1. The Manager clicks "Save & Add another" (Lưu & Thêm tiếp).
2. The system saves the current supplier and resets the modal form to allow adding the next one immediately.

Alternative Flow 2: Validation Error
1. The Manager leaves required fields (e.g., Supplier Name) empty.
2. The system displays an error message preventing submission until corrected.

Alternative Flow 3: Cancel Creation
1. The Manager clicks the "Cancel" (Hủy) button.
2. The system discards all inputs and closes the modal.
2.5.4 UC 23 - Update Supplier
Primary Actors
Manager
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Manager, I want to modify an existing supplier's information to keep their profile, contacts, and bank details up to date.
Preconditions
The Manager must be logged into the system, and the target supplier must exist.
Postconditions
The selected supplier's details are updated in the system.
Normal Sequence/Flow
1. From the Supplier list or Details page, the Manager clicks the "Edit" (Chỉnh sửa) button for a supplier.
2. The system opens the "Edit Supplier" modal populated with the current data across all tabs.
3. The Manager modifies the desired fields.
4. The Manager clicks the "Update" (Cập nhật) button.
5. The system validates the input, updates the record, closes the modal, and displays a success notification.
6. The system refreshes the data to show the updated supplier information.
Alternative Sequences/Flows
Alternative Flow 1: Validation Error
1. The Manager leaves required fields empty.
2. The system displays an error message preventing the update.

Alternative Flow 2: Cancel Update
1. The Manager clicks the "Cancel" (Hủy) button.
2. The system discards any changes made and closes the modal.

2.5.5 UC 24 - Delete Supplier
Primary Actors
Manager
Secondary Actors
None
Description
As a Manager, I want to remove a supplier from the system when we no longer do business with them, provided they have no active transactions or outstanding debt.
Preconditions
The Manager must be logged into the system. The target supplier must not have active purchase orders or unpaid debts preventing deletion.
Postconditions
The selected supplier is permanently removed from the system.
Normal Sequence/Flow
1. From the Supplier list or Details page, the Manager clicks the "Delete" (Xóa) icon/button.
2. The system displays a confirmation modal warning the user that the action cannot be undone ("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa nhà cung cấp... Hành động này không thể hoàn tác").
3. The Manager clicks the "Confirm Delete" (Xác nhận xóa) button.
4. The system deletes the supplier record, closes the modal, and displays a success notification.
5. The system refreshes the list to remove the deleted supplier.
Alternative Sequences/Flows
Alternative Flow 1: Cancel Deletion
1. In the confirmation modal, the Manager clicks the "Cancel" (Hủy) button.
2. The system closes the modal without deleting the supplier.

Alternative Flow 2: Deletion Prevented
1. If the supplier has outstanding debt or linked transactions, the system displays an error message preventing deletion and suggests settling debts first.
2.6 Customer Management
2.6.1 UC 25 - View Customer List
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to view customer list so that I can review accurate customer management information for daily operations and decision-making. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Customer Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The requested customer data is displayed successfully.
- No business data is modified by this use case. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to access Customer Management.
3. The user navigates to the Customer List screen.
4. The system retrieves customer records from the database.
5. The system displays the list with search, filter, sort, and pagination controls.
6. The user reviews the list or selects a record for further action. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 4.1 - No data found
1. The system cannot find any customer records matching the current condition.
2. The system displays an empty-state message such as "No data found".
3. The user changes the search/filter condition or creates a new record if permitted.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Invalid search or filter condition
1. The user enters a search keyword or filter value in an invalid format or exceeding the allowed length.
2. The system highlights the invalid condition and displays a validation message.
3. The user corrects the condition.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow. 

2.6.2 UC 26 -View Customer Detail
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff 	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager or Staff member, I want to view a customer's detailed profile, contact details, payment terms, and ledger history so that I can manage client accounts and track outstanding debts. 
Preconditions	1. The user must be logged in with appropriate permission (e.g., customer:view).
2. The user must navigate to the Customer page (/customers).
Postconditions	The system renders the customer details drawer (CustomerDrawer) containing three sections: General Info, Debt History, and Transaction History.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks on a customer row in the table, or clicks the eye icon (icon bi-eye) in the action column.
2.	The system opens the CustomerDrawer sliding from the right.
3.	The system triggers an API request to the backend: GET /api/v1/partners/{id}.
4.	The backend queries the database (PARTNERS table), joins related tables, and returns a 200 OK response.
5.	The system renders the customer details on the drawer:
●	Customer Name, Customer Code, Customer Type Badge, and Status Badge in the header.
●	A tab menu with three buttons: "Thông tin chung", "Lịch sử công nợ", and "Lịch sử giao dịch".
6.	The user views the default active "Thông tin chung" tab displaying:
●	Số điện thoại (Phone), Email, Địa chỉ (Address), Mã số thuế (Tax Code)
●	Hạn mức công nợ (Credit Limit), Số ngày được nợ (Payment Term Days)
●	Nhóm khách hàng (Group Type: RETAIL, WHOLESALE, DISTRIBUTOR)
●	Tài khoản ngân hàng (Account Number, Bank Name, Beneficiary Name)
7.	The user clicks the "Lịch sử công nợ" (Debt History) tab. The system updates the view to show a table of ledger records (from PARTNER_LEDGER) showing debt increases, receipts, and balances after.
8.	The user clicks the "Lịch sử giao dịch" (Transaction History) tab. The system updates the view to show a list of sales orders (from SALES_ORDERS) associated with this customer.
9.	The user clicks the close button (icon bi-x-lg) or clicks outside the drawer on the overlay.
10.	The system closes the drawer.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Click Customer Code link
1.	In the customer list table, the user clicks the customer code link (e.g. KH-0001).
2.	The system opens the CustomerDrawer directly. Execution continues from step 3 of the Normal Flow.
Alternative Flow 2: Transition to Edit mode
1.	Inside the customer details drawer, the user clicks the button "Chỉnh sửa" in the footer.
2.	The system turns the customer details into input fields, allowing updates to contact details, tax code, bank info, and credit limits.
3.	The user updates the fields and clicks "Lưu thay đổi". The system saves the updates via API PUT /api/v1/partners/{id} and returns to read-only mode.
Alternative Flow 3: Error Loading Customer details
1.	If the API request to fetch details fails (e.g., database connection timeout or resource not found), the system displays an error alert: "Không thể lấy thông tin khách hàng." and keeps the drawer closed.

2.6.3 UC 27 - Create Customer
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to create customer so that new customer management information can be recorded and tracked in the system. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Customer Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- A new customer record is created successfully.
- The creation action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related inventory, serial, warranty, or report data is updated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to create customer records.
3. The user navigates to the Customer List screen.
4. The user clicks the Create/New button.
5. The system displays the Customer creation form.
6. The user enters all required information.
7. The user clicks Save.
8. The system validates mandatory fields, data format, and uniqueness rules.
9. The system saves the new customer record to the database.
10. The system records the creation action in the Activity Log.
11. The system displays a success message and redirects the user to the Customer Detail screen or List screen. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 6.1 - Missing mandatory information
1. The user leaves one or more required fields blank.
2. The system prevents saving and displays validation messages next to the missing fields.
3. The user enters the missing information.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - Invalid data format
1. The system detects an invalid input format such as wrong email format, invalid phone number, negative quantity, invalid date, or text exceeding maximum length.
2. The system highlights the invalid fields and displays the relevant validation messages.
3. The user corrects the invalid information.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The system detects duplicated information such as code, name, serial number, email, phone number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Save failed
1. The database or backend service cannot save the record.
2. The system displays a failure message and keeps the entered data on the screen.
3. The user retries after the issue is resolved.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow. 

2.6.4 UC 28 - Update Customer
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to update customer so that incorrect or changed customer management information can be corrected while preserving traceability. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Customer Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The customer information is updated successfully.
- The update action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related business data is revalidated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to update customer records.
3. The user navigates to the Customer List screen and searches for the record.
4. The user opens the record detail screen.
5. The user clicks Edit/Update.
6. The system displays the Customer update form with current data pre-filled.
7. The user modifies the needed information.
8. The user clicks Save.
9. The system validates mandatory fields, data format, uniqueness rules, and business constraints.
10. The system saves the updated customer information.
11. The system records the update action in the Activity Log.
12. The system displays a success message and returns the user to the detail screen or list screen. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 3.1 - Record not found
1. The search condition does not match any record or the selected record no longer exists.
2. The system displays "Record not found".
3. The user returns to the list and searches again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Record is locked or not editable
1. The system checks the record status.
2. The record is already posted, cancelled, deleted, inactive, or linked to a locked transaction.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a message explaining why the record cannot be updated.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Invalid or missing updated data
1. The system detects missing required fields, invalid format, invalid date range, or text exceeding maximum length.
2. The system displays validation errors.
3. The user corrects the data.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The updated value duplicates an existing code, name, email, phone number, serial number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Concurrent update conflict
1. Another user updates the same record before the current user saves.
2. The system detects the data version conflict.
3. The system asks the user to reload the latest data before saving again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow. 

2.6.5 UC 29 - Delete Customer
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with delete permission, I want to delete customer so that obsolete customer management master data can be removed or deactivated safely. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Customer Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The customer record is deleted or marked inactive successfully.
- The delete/deactivate action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related historical transaction data remains traceable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to delete or deactivate customer records.
3. The user navigates to the Customer List screen and searches for the record.
4. The user selects the record and clicks Delete/Deactivate.
5. The system displays a confirmation dialog.
6. The user confirms the action.
7. The system validates that the record has no active dependencies or restricted status.
8. The system deletes the customer record or changes its status to Inactive.
9. The system records the delete/deactivate action in the Activity Log.
10. The system displays a success message and refreshes the list. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 5.1 - User cancels deletion
1. The user chooses Cancel in the confirmation dialog.
2. The system closes the dialog.
3. No data is changed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.1 - Record has linked transactions
1. The system finds that the record is referenced by import slips, export slips, sales orders, warranties, reports, or other historical documents.
2. The system blocks physical deletion to preserve traceability.
3. The system displays a message and suggests deactivation if allowed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.2 - Record is already inactive or not found
1. The selected record has already been deleted/inactivated or no longer exists.
2. The system displays a relevant message.
3. The system refreshes the list.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.

2.7.2 UC 31 - View Product Detail
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff 	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager or Staff member, I want to view a product's full specifications, images, variants with prices, and detailed stock balances in different warehouses so that I can inspect inventory catalogs and assist in procurement or sales operations. 
Preconditions	1. The user must be logged in with view permission (e.g., product:view).
2. The user must navigate to the Product page (/products). 
Postconditions	The system renders the product details drawer (ProductDetailDrawer) displaying general attributes, variant listings, and warehouse stocks. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks on the product code link (codeCell) in the products table.
2.	The system opens the ProductDetailDrawer sliding in from the right.
3.	The system calls backend endpoint: GET /api/v1/products/{id}.
4.	The backend retrieves the product details, variants, images, and inventory balance from database (joining tables PRODUCTS, PRODUCT_VARIANTS, INVENTORY_BALANCES) and returns 200 OK.
5.	The system renders the drawer:
●	Product Image (or placeholder), Product Name (productName), Product Code (productCode), and active status badge in the header.
●	A tab navigation bar: "Thông tin chung", "Các phiên bản", and "Tồn kho chi tiết".
6.	The user inspects the active "Thông tin chung" (General Info) tab, displaying:
●	Tính chất (Product Type: Hàng hóa / Dịch vụ / Thành phẩm)
●	Thương hiệu (Brand Name), Nhóm danh mục (Category Name)
●	Đơn vị tính (Unit Name), Giảm thuế theo quy định (Tax Reduction Status)
●	Mô tả chi tiết (Detailed Description)
●	Thiết lập quản lý: Checkboxes for Serial tracking (track_serial) and Lot tracking (track_lot).
7.	The user clicks the "Các phiên bản" (Variants) tab. The system renders a table listing product variants (from PRODUCT_VARIANTS) displaying:
●	Mã SKU (SKU Code), Tên phiên bản (Variant Name), Giá vốn (Cost Price), Giá bán (Sale Price), and Thông số kỹ thuật (Specs).
8.	The user clicks the "Tồn kho chi tiết" (Detailed Inventory) tab. The system renders a table of stock balances (from INVENTORY_BALANCES) showing:
●	Kho hàng (Warehouse name), Số lượng tồn thực tế (Quantity on Hand), Số lượng giữ hàng (Reserved Quantity).
9.	The user clicks the close button (icon bi-x-lg) or clicks outside on the overlay.
10.	The system closes the drawer.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Edit from product details view
1.	Inside the details drawer, the user clicks the "Sửa" button in the footer.
2.	The system closes the drawer and displays the product edit modal (showModal = true) containing the form inputs.
3.	The user modifies inputs and clicks the "Cất" (Save) button. The system submits changes via PUT /api/v1/products/{id} and reloads the table.
Alternative Flow 2: Error Loading Product details
1.	If the API request fails, the system displays an error alert: "Không thể tải chi tiết hàng hóa." and the drawer stays closed.

2.7.6 UC 35 - Print Bar Code
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	Barcode Printer 
Description	As a Manager or Staff member, I want to print barcode labels for products so that warehouse staff can identify and handle items more quickly. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Product Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- Barcode labels are generated and sent to the printer successfully.
- The printing action is recorded if print auditing is enabled. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to print barcodes.
3. The user navigates to the Product List or Product Detail screen.
4. The user selects one or more products/variants/serial numbers for barcode printing.
5. The user selects barcode template, label quantity, and printer if applicable.
6. The user clicks Print Barcode.
7. The system validates selected products, barcode data, and label quantity.
8. The system generates barcode labels.
9. The system sends the labels to the barcode printer or exports them as a printable file.
10. The system displays a success message. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 4.1 - No product selected
1. The user clicks Print Barcode without selecting any product, variant, or serial number.
2. The system displays a validation message.
3. The user selects at least one item.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.1 - Missing barcode data
1. The selected item has no valid product code, SKU, or serial number for barcode generation.
2. The system displays a missing-data message.
3. The user updates the product/variant/serial information before printing.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Printer unavailable
1. The barcode printer is disconnected, unavailable, or returns an error.
2. The system displays a printer error message.
3. The user selects another printer or exports the barcode labels as a file.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow. 
 

2.8 Brand Management
2.8.1 UC 36 - View Brand List
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to view brand list so that I can review accurate brand management information for daily operations and decision-making. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Brand Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The requested brand data is displayed successfully.
- No business data is modified by this use case. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to access Brand Management.
3. The user navigates to the Brand List screen.
4. The system retrieves brand records from the database.
5. The system displays the list with search, filter, sort, and pagination controls.
6. The user reviews the list or selects a record for further action. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 4.1 - No data found
1. The system cannot find any brand records matching the current condition.
2. The system displays an empty-state message such as "No data found".
3. The user changes the search/filter condition or creates a new record if permitted.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Invalid search or filter condition
1. The user enters a search keyword or filter value in an invalid format or exceeding the allowed length.
2. The system highlights the invalid condition and displays a validation message.
3. The user corrects the condition.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow. 

2.8. UC 37 - View Brand Details
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff 	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager or Staff member, I want to view detailed information of a Brand in the catalog to check its status and see how many products are linked to this Brand. 
Preconditions	1. The user must be logged in with catalog view permissions.
2. The user must navigate to the Brands settings page (/brands). 
Postconditions	The system renders the BrandDetailModal popup containing Brand attributes and product counters. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks on a Brand name in the table or clicks the eye icon in the table functions.
2.	The system opens the BrandDetailModal pop-up.
3.	The system issues a request: GET /api/v1/brands/{id}.
4.	The backend retrieves the Brand records (from BRANDS table) along with a count of related products in the PRODUCTS table, and returns a 200 OK response.
5.	The system displays the Brand details:
●	Mã thương hiệu (Brand Code)
●	Tên thương hiệu (Brand Name)
●	Trạng thái (Status: Đang sử dụng / Ngừng sử dụng)
●	Ngày tạo (Created Date)
●	Tổng sản phẩm liên kết (Total Associated Products count)
6.	The user clicks the "Đóng" (Close) button or the "X" close icon in the header.
7.	The system closes the modal.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Edit Brand from details modal
1.	Inside the details modal, the user clicks the "Sửa" (Edit) button.
2.	The system turns the brand name text field into an input field.
3.	The user updates the name and clicks "Cất" (Save). The system saves updates via API PUT /api/v1/brands/{id}, reloads the catalog table, and closes the modal.
Alternative Flow 2: Error Loading Brand
1.	If the API call fails, the system shows an error alert: "Không thể lấy thông tin thương hiệu." and keeps the modal closed.

2.8. UC 38 - Create Brand
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to create brand so that new brand management information can be recorded and tracked in the system. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Brand Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- A new brand record is created successfully.
- The creation action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related inventory, serial, warranty, or report data is updated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to create brand records.
3. The user navigates to the Brand List screen.
4. The user clicks the Create/New button.
5. The system displays the Brand creation form.
6. The user enters all required information.
7. The user clicks Save.
8. The system validates mandatory fields, data format, and uniqueness rules.
9. The system saves the new brand record to the database.
10. The system records the creation action in the Activity Log.
11. The system displays a success message and redirects the user to the Brand Detail screen or List screen. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 6.1 - Missing mandatory information
1. The user leaves one or more required fields blank.
2. The system prevents saving and displays validation messages next to the missing fields.
3. The user enters the missing information.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - Invalid data format
1. The system detects an invalid input format such as wrong email format, invalid phone number, negative quantity, invalid date, or text exceeding maximum length.
2. The system highlights the invalid fields and displays the relevant validation messages.
3. The user corrects the invalid information.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The system detects duplicated information such as code, name, serial number, email, phone number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Save failed
1. The database or backend service cannot save the record.
2. The system displays a failure message and keeps the entered data on the screen.
3. The user retries after the issue is resolved.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow. 

2.8. UC 39 - Update Brand
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to update brand so that incorrect or changed brand management information can be corrected while preserving traceability. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Brand Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The brand information is updated successfully.
- The update action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related business data is revalidated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to update brand records.
3. The user navigates to the Brand List screen and searches for the record.
4. The user opens the record detail screen.
5. The user clicks Edit/Update.
6. The system displays the Brand update form with current data pre-filled.
7. The user modifies the needed information.
8. The user clicks Save.
9. The system validates mandatory fields, data format, uniqueness rules, and business constraints.
10. The system saves the updated brand information.
11. The system records the update action in the Activity Log.
12. The system displays a success message and returns the user to the detail screen or list screen. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 3.1 - Record not found
1. The search condition does not match any record or the selected record no longer exists.
2. The system displays "Record not found".
3. The user returns to the list and searches again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Record is locked or not editable
1. The system checks the record status.
2. The record is already posted, cancelled, deleted, inactive, or linked to a locked transaction.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a message explaining why the record cannot be updated.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Invalid or missing updated data
1. The system detects missing required fields, invalid format, invalid date range, or text exceeding maximum length.
2. The system displays validation errors.
3. The user corrects the data.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The updated value duplicates an existing code, name, email, phone number, serial number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Concurrent update conflict
1. Another user updates the same record before the current user saves.
2. The system detects the data version conflict.
3. The system asks the user to reload the latest data before saving again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow. 

2.8. UC 40 - Delete Brand
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with delete permission, I want to delete brand so that obsolete brand management master data can be removed or deactivated safely. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Brand Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The brand record is deleted or marked inactive successfully.
- The delete/deactivate action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related historical transaction data remains traceable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to delete or deactivate brand records.
3. The user navigates to the Brand List screen and searches for the record.
4. The user selects the record and clicks Delete/Deactivate.
5. The system displays a confirmation dialog.
6. The user confirms the action.
7. The system validates that the record has no active dependencies or restricted status.
8. The system deletes the brand record or changes its status to Inactive.
9. The system records the delete/deactivate action in the Activity Log.
10. The system displays a success message and refreshes the list. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 5.1 - User cancels deletion
1. The user chooses Cancel in the confirmation dialog.
2. The system closes the dialog.
3. No data is changed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.1 - Record has linked transactions
1. The system finds that the record is referenced by import slips, export slips, sales orders, warranties, reports, or other historical documents.
2. The system blocks physical deletion to preserve traceability.
3. The system displays a message and suggests deactivation if allowed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.2 - Record is already inactive or not found
1. The selected record has already been deleted/inactivated or no longer exists.
2. The system displays a relevant message.
3. The system refreshes the list.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.

2.9. UC 42 - View Category Details
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff 	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager or Staff member, I want to view the details of a Product Category, including its hierarchical parent category, status, and creation date, so that I can inspect the taxonomy of the catalog. 
Preconditions	1. The user must be logged in with catalog view permissions.
2. The user must navigate to the Categories settings page (/categories). 
Postconditions	The system renders the CategoryDetailModal pop-up showing category attributes and parent relations. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks on a Category name in the table or clicks the eye icon in the actions list.
2.	The system opens the CategoryDetailModal.
3.	The system makes a backend request: GET /api/v1/categories/{id}.
4.	The backend queries the PRODUCT_CATEGORIES table, joins the table with itself to fetch the parent category name, and returns a 200 OK response.
5.	The system renders:
●	Mã danh mục (Category Code)
●	Tên danh mục (Category Name)
●	Danh mục cha (Parent Category Name, shows "Không có" if parent_id is null)
●	Trạng thái (Status: Đang sử dụng / Ngừng sử dụng)
●	Ngày tạo (Created Date)
6.	The user clicks the "Đóng" (Close) button or the "X" close icon in the header.
7.	The system closes the modal.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Edit Category details
1.	Inside the details modal, the user clicks the "Sửa" (Edit) button.
2.	The system turns details fields into inputs, including a dropdown select box to change the parent category.
3.	The user updates the fields and clicks "Cất" (Save). The system sends a PUT /api/v1/categories/{id} request, reloads the table, and closes the modal.
Alternative Flow 2: Error Loading Category
1.	If the API request fails, the system shows an error alert: "Không thể lấy thông tin danh mục." and keeps the modal closed.
