3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 3.1 - Record not found
1. The search condition does not match any record or the selected record no longer exists.
2. The system displays "Record not found".
3. The user returns to the list and searches again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Record is locked or not editable
1. The system checks the record status.
2. The record is already posted, cancelled, deleted, inactive, or linked to a locked transaction.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a message explaining why the record cannot be updated.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Invalid or missing updated data
1. The system detects missing required fields, invalid format, invalid date range, or text exceeding maximum length.
2. The system displays validation errors.
3. The user corrects the data.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The updated value duplicates an existing code, name, email, phone number, serial number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Concurrent update conflict
1. Another user updates the same record before the current user saves.
2. The system detects the data version conflict.
3. The system asks the user to reload the latest data before saving again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow. 

2.10.4 UC 49 -  Delete Unit 
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with delete permission, I want to delete unit so that obsolete unit management master data can be removed or deactivated safely. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Unit Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The unit record is deleted or marked inactive successfully.
- The delete/deactivate action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related historical transaction data remains traceable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to delete or deactivate unit records.
3. The user navigates to the Unit List screen and searches for the record.
4. The user selects the record and clicks Delete/Deactivate.
5. The system displays a confirmation dialog.
6. The user confirms the action.
7. The system validates that the record has no active dependencies or restricted status.
8. The system deletes the unit record or changes its status to Inactive.
9. The system records the delete/deactivate action in the Activity Log.
10. The system displays a success message and refreshes the list. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 5.1 - User cancels deletion
1. The user chooses Cancel in the confirmation dialog.
2. The system closes the dialog.
3. No data is changed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.1 - Record has linked transactions
1. The system finds that the record is referenced by import slips, export slips, sales orders, warranties, reports, or other historical documents.
2. The system blocks physical deletion to preserve traceability.
3. The system displays a message and suggests deactivation if allowed.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.2 - Record is already inactive or not found
1. The selected record has already been deleted/inactivated or no longer exists.
2. The system displays a relevant message.
3. The system refreshes the list.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow. 

2.11 Inventory Import Management
2.11.1 UC 50 - View Import History
Primary Actors	Manager, Staff	Secondary Actors	System Database
Description	As a Manager or Staff, I want to view the list of import slips so that I can track incoming inventory, monitor slip statuses, and review total amounts imported.
Preconditions	1. The actor is authenticated and logged into the DLC-WMS system.
2. The actor has the functional permission to "View Import Orders".
Postconditions	1. The system displays a paginated list of import slips based on the applied filters.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor navigates to the "Kho hàng" or "Danh sách đơn" menu and selects the "Nhập kho" tab.
2. The system retrieves and displays the default paginated list of import slips.
3. The actor inputs filter criteria: "Từ ngày" (From Date), "Đến ngày" (To Date), or "Tình trạng" (Status - e.g., Hoàn thành, Chờ duyệt, Đã hủy) and clicks "Lọc dữ liệu".
4. The system updates the table to display matching records. The table columns include: Ngày ghi nhận, Số phiếu, Đối tác, Kho hàng, Tổng tiền, Tình trạng, Thao tác.
5. The actor can click the pagination controls (1, 2, 3...) at the bottom to navigate through records. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Clear Filters
1. In step 3, the actor clicks "Làm mới" (Refresh/Clear).
2. The system clears all search/filter inputs and reloads the default list.

Alternative Flow 2: No Records Found
1. In step 4, if the filter criteria yield no results, the system displays an empty state message (e.g., "No data available"). 

2.11.2 UC 51- Create Import Slip
Primary Actors	Manager, Staff	Secondary Actors	System Database
Description	As a Manager or Staff, I want to create a new import slip so that I can record incoming products from suppliers and update the warehouse inventory.
Preconditions	1. The actor must be logged into the DLC-WMS system.
2. The actor must have the "Create Import Orders" permission.
3. Master data (Products, Suppliers, Warehouses) must exist in the system.
Postconditions	1. A new import slip is saved in the database with a unique ID and "Pending" or "Completed" status.
2. If completed, the inventory quantities for the imported products are increased in the selected warehouse.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor clicks the "+ Thêm mới" (Add New) button on the Import History page.
2. The system displays the Create Import Slip form.
3. The actor selects the Supplier, Delivery Place, and Destination Warehouse.
4. The actor adds products to the slip by selecting from the product list and entering the quantity and unit price.
5. The system calculates and displays the total amount dynamically.
6. The actor reviews the details and clicks "Lưu" (Save) or "Hoàn thành" (Complete).
7. The system validates the input, saves the import slip, updates the inventory (if completed), and displays a success message.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Missing Required Information
1. At step 6, the actor leaves mandatory fields (e.g., Supplier, Warehouse, or Products) blank.
2. The system prevents saving, highlights the empty fields in red, and displays an error message ("Vui lòng điền đầy đủ thông tin").

Alternative Flow 2: Invalid Quantity or Price
1. The actor inputs a negative value or zero for quantity/price.
2. The system immediately shows a validation error and disables the Save button until corrected.

2.11.3 UC 52- View Import Slip Detail
Primary Actors	Manager, Staff		Secondary Actors	System Database
Description	User Story: As a Manager or Staff, I want to view the full detailed information of a specific import slip so that I can verify the exact products, quantities, supplier info, and total amount of that transaction.	
Preconditions	1. The actor must be logged into the DLC-WMS system.
2. The actor must have the "View Import Orders" permission.
3. The specific import slip must exist in the system.
Postconditions	1. The system displays the detailed information of the selected import slip without altering any data.	
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor is on the "Danh sách hàng nhập" (Import History) page.
2. The actor clicks the View icon (Eye icon) corresponding to a specific import slip record.
3. The system retrieves the slip's detailed data from the database.
4. The system displays the "Chi tiết Đơn nhập hàng" (Import Slip Details) panel or modal.
5. The actor views the General Info (Supplier, Address, Receiving Date, Warehouse, Receiver) and the Product List (Product Code, Name, Unit, Quantity, Import Price, Total Price).
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Record Deleted or Not Found
1. At step 2, the actor clicks View, but the record was recently deleted by another user.
2. The system displays an error message ("Record not found or has been deleted") and refreshes the list.

Alternative Flow 2: Network Error
1. At step 3, the system fails to retrieve data due to a database/network timeout.
2. The system displays a "Connection Error" notification and prompts the actor to try again.

2.11.4 UC 53- Update Import Slip
Primary Actors	Manager, Staff	Secondary Actors	System Database
Description	User Story: As a Manager or Staff, I want to edit an existing import slip (that has not yet been completed/locked) so that I can correct mistakes in product quantities, prices, or supplier information before it affects the actual warehouse inventory.	
Preconditions	1. The actor must be logged into the DLC-WMS system.
2. The actor must have the "Edit Import Orders" permission.
3. The target import slip must exist and its status must be "Draft" or "Pending" (not "Completed" or "Cancelled").
Postconditions	1. The import slip is successfully updated in the database with the new information.
2. The system logs the update action in the system history.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The actor is on the "Danh sách hàng nhập" (Import History) page.
2. The actor clicks the Edit icon (Pencil icon) for a specific "Pending" import slip.
3. The system retrieves the slip's current data and opens the "Cập nhật Đơn nhập hàng" (Update Import Slip) form.
4. The actor modifies the necessary fields (e.g., changes quantity, adds/removes products, or updates supplier).
5. The system dynamically recalculates the total amount.
6. The actor clicks "Lưu thay đổi" (Save Changes).
7. The system validates the input, updates the record in the database, and displays a success notification ("Cập nhật thành công").
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Attempt to Edit a Completed Slip
1. The actor attempts to edit an import slip that has already been marked as "Completed".
2. The system disables the edit action and shows a warning: "Cannot edit a completed import slip. Inventory has already been updated."

Alternative Flow 2: Validation Failure during Update
1. At step 6, the actor saves but left a mandatory field empty or entered an invalid quantity.
2. The system prevents the update and highlights the erroneous fields in red.

2.12 Inventory Export Management
2.12.1 UC 54 -  View Export History List
Primary Actors	Manager, Staff	Secondary Actor	None
Description	As a staff member, I want to create export slips, so that stock can leave the warehouse for valid business purposes.
Preconditions	User account has been created & authorized
Postconditions	●	User logs in the system successfully
●	The system tracked successful login into the Activity Log
Normal Sequence/Flow	Login System
1.	The user login to the system with his/her account.
2.	System redirects user to the Home page 
3.	User navigates to Warehouse -> Export Slip 
4.	The system shows the Export Slip List to the user.
5.	User click to the “Thêm” button
6.	User fills in generation information of of the export slip 
7.	User enters line details of exported items
8.	User reviews information and click saves
9.	System checks inventory and business rules
10.	System creates the Export slip and update status
11.	 System logs the export slip creation to Activity Log.
12.	 The system displays a success message and returns the user to the slip list or previous page.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1_Google Login
1.	User clicks Google Login button to login system using Google account
2.	System redirects the user to the Google’s Login screen
3.	User types in the Google account details and chooses to login
4.	Google validates user’s login information successfully and redirect him/her back to the system
5.	Return to step 5 of normal flow.
Step 4_System can’t authenticate the user
User can’t be authenticated & get relevant error message in one of below cases
1.	He/she leaves the Email and/or Password field blank (MSG10)
2.	Input Email or Password are incorrect (MSG09)
3.	Input Email/Password are correct but email has not been verified (MSG11)
4.	The user account is blocked / inactive (MSG12)
If user inputs wrong logging-in details 5 times continuously, system will lock his/her account in 30 minutes (with relevant warning message - MSG13)
Step 13.1_ Insufficient Inventory
1.	When checking inventory, system find the export amount is larger than amount inventory
2.	System display error message “Số lượng xuất lớn hơn số lượng tồn kho”, vui lòng điều chỉnh”
3.	Users edit the export amount.
4.	Return to step 7

2.12.3 UC 56 - View Export Slip Details
Primary Actors	Manager/Staff	Secondary Actor	None
Description	As a manager / staff, I want to view details of an export slip so that I can know information about that slip.
Preconditions	●	User account has been created & authorized
●	Export Slips have been created and saved on the list
●	The user has logged into the system successfully.
Postconditions	●	The export slip details are displayed successfully.
●	The user can review the slip information without modifying it in this use case.
●	The viewing action can be recorded in the Activity Log if your system tracks audit events.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user logs into the system successfully.
2.	The user navigates to the Export Slip List screen.
3.	The system displays a list of available export slips.
4.	The user searches or filters the list by slip number, date, status, warehouse, or related object.
5.	The user selects one export slip from the list.
6.	The system displays the Export Slip Details page.
7.	The system shows the export slip header information, such as slip number, export type, created date, creator, warehouse, and status.
8.	The system shows the slip line items, including product code, product name, quantity, unit price, total amount, and serial/lot if applicable.
9.	The system shows related information such as approval history, confirmation history, attached files, and notes if available.
10.	The user reviews the information and leaves the page or returns to the list

Alternative Sequences/Flows	A1. Export slip not found
1.	The user selects a slip from the list or enters a direct slip reference.
2.	The system cannot find the export slip.
3.	The system displays a message such as: “Export slip not found or no longer available.”
4.	The user is returned to the export slip list.
A3. Slip is deleted or canceled
1.	The user opens a slip that has been canceled or deleted.
2.	The system still displays the basic details if audit history is kept, but shows the slip status as Canceled or Deleted.
3.	The user is not allowed to perform editing actions from this screen.
A4. Serial or lot-based items
1.	The slip contains items managed by serial number or lot.
2.	The system displays the serial/lot information for each item line.
3.	If serial tracking is unavailable for a line, the system shows a blank or “N/A” state for that field.


2.12.4 UC 57 - Update Export Slip
Primary Actors	Manager / Staff	Secondary Actor	None
Description	As a manager / staff, I can update an export slip so that I can correct slip’s wrong information 
Preconditions	●	User accounts have been created and authorized.
●	The user has logged into the system successfully.
●	The system tracked the successful login in the Activity Log.
●	An export slip already exists in the export slip list.
Postconditions	●	The export slip information is updated and saved successfully.
●	The system records the update action in the Activity Log.
●	If the update changes stock-related data, the system revalidates quantity and serial/lot availability.
●	If the slip was already finalized, the system does not allow direct update and redirects to adjustment or cancellation flow.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	Manager or Staff logs into the system successfully.
2.	The user navigates to the Export Slip List screen.
3.	The user searches for the export slip that needs to be updated.
4.	The user opens the slip detail page.
5.	The user clicks Edit / Update.
6.	The system checks whether the user has permission to edit the slip.
7.	The system checks whether the slip is still editable.
8.	The system displays the export slip form with current data pre-filled.
9.	The user updates the needed information, such as warehouse, products, quantity, unit price, serial/lot, note, or attachments.
10.	The user clicks Save.
11.	The system validates the updated information.
12.	The system checks stock availability and serial/lot validity again if the changed fields affect inventory.
13.	The system saves the updated export slip.
14.	The system updates the Activity Log with the edit action.
15.	The system returns the user to the slip detail page or the export slip list with a success message.

Alternative Sequences/Flows	A1. Slip is not editable
1.	The user opens the export slip.
2.	The system checks the slip status.
3.	If the slip is already Approved, Confirmed, Exported, or Canceled, the system blocks direct editing.
4.	The system displays a message such as “This export slip can no longer be updated. Please use adjustment or cancellation workflow.”
5.	The user is redirected to the proper workflow.

A2. Invalid quantity or missing serial
1.	The user changes product quantity or serial information.
2.	The system validates the updated values.
3.	If quantity exceeds available stock or required serial/lot is missing, the system rejects the save.
4.	The system displays validation errors.
5.	The user corrects the data and saves again.
A3. Slip is linked to another document
1.	The user tries to update a slip that is linked to a sale order, invoice, transfer, or approval record.
2.	The system detects the dependency.
3.	The system blocks restricted fields from being edited or blocks the whole update, depending on configuration.
4.	The system informs the user that the slip is locked due to linked records.
A4. Session expires
1.	The user stays on the edit page too long.
2.	The session expires before save.
3.	The system redirects the user to the login page.
4.	After re-login, the user must reopen the export slip.

2.13.2 UC 59 -  Create Transfer Slip
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None 
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to create transfer slip so that new stock transfer management information can be recorded and tracked in the system. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Stock Transfer Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- A new transfer slip record is created successfully.
- The creation action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related inventory, serial, warranty, or report data is updated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to create transfer slips.
3. The user navigates to the Stock Transfer History/List screen.
4. The user clicks Create Transfer Slip.
5. The system displays the transfer slip form.
6. The user selects source warehouse, destination warehouse, transfer date, and note.
7. The user adds transfer line items including product/variant, quantity, unit, and serial/lot information if applicable.
8. The user reviews the transfer slip and clicks Save or Submit.
9. The system validates mandatory fields, warehouse relationship, stock availability, quantity, and serial/lot availability.
10. The system saves the transfer slip with the proper status.
11. If the slip is posted, the system deducts stock from the source warehouse and increases stock in the destination warehouse.
12. The system records the creation action in the Activity Log.
13. The system displays a success message and returns to the transfer detail or list. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 6.1 - Source and destination warehouse are the same
1. The user selects the same warehouse as both source and destination.
2. The system displays a warehouse validation message.
3. The user selects different warehouses.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 7.1 - Invalid transfer line
1. The user enters a zero/negative quantity, invalid product, or invalid unit.
2. The system highlights the invalid line and displays validation messages.
3. The user corrects the line item.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Insufficient source stock
1. The requested transfer quantity exceeds available stock in the source warehouse.
2. The system displays an insufficient-stock message.
3. The user reduces the quantity or selects another source warehouse.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Missing or invalid serial/lot
1. The transferred product requires serial/lot tracking but the selected serial/lot is missing or unavailable.
2. The system rejects the save/post action.
3. The user selects valid serial/lot information.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.

Step 5.2 - Transfer slip is already posted or cancelled
1. The system checks the transfer slip status.
2. The slip is Posted or Cancelled, so direct editing is not allowed.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a status-related message.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.3 - Invalid warehouse or insufficient stock after update
1. The user changes source warehouse, destination warehouse, product, serial/lot, or quantity.
2. The system revalidates warehouses and available source stock.
3. The system rejects the save action if the updated information violates business rules.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.

2.14.2 UC 63 -  Create Stocktake
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to create stocktake so that new stocktake management information can be recorded and tracked in the system.
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Stocktake Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally.
Postconditions	- A new stocktake record is created successfully.
- The creation action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related inventory, serial, warranty, or report data is updated when applicable.
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to create stocktake sessions.
3. The user navigates to the Stocktake History/List screen.
4. The user clicks Create Stocktake.
5. The system displays the stocktake creation form.
6. The user selects warehouse, stocktake date, scope, reference document if needed, and note.
7. The user adds or imports stocktake line items.
8. The user enters counted quantities for each item.
9. The user clicks Save or Submit.
10. The system validates mandatory fields, counted quantity, reference data, and item uniqueness.
11. The system calculates difference quantities between system quantity and counted quantity.
12. The system saves the stocktake session.
13. The system records the creation action in the Activity Log.
14. The system displays a success message and returns to stocktake detail or list.
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 6.1 - Missing warehouse or stocktake date
1. The user leaves required header information blank.
2. The system displays required-field validation messages.
3. The user enters the missing information.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - Invalid counted quantity
1. The user enters a negative counted quantity or a non-numeric value.
2. The system displays a counted-quantity validation message.
3. The user corrects the quantity.
Return to step 8 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Duplicate stocktake line
1. The same product/variant/serial/lot appears more than once in the stocktake lines.
2. The system highlights duplicated lines and prevents saving.
3. The user merges or removes duplicated lines.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 11.1 - Difference requires review
1. The calculated difference exceeds the configured threshold.
2. The system marks the line for review or requires a manager confirmation before posting.
3. The user reviews the difference and continues according to permission.
Return to step 8 of Normal Flow.

7. The user selects one reference document.
8. The system validates whether the selected document can be used as a stocktake reference.

10. The user continues creating or updating the stocktake session.

Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - Invalid reference document status

2. The system blocks the selection and displays a validation message.
3. The user selects another document.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.2 - Warehouse mismatch
1. The selected reference document belongs to a different warehouse from the stocktake session.
2. The system displays a warehouse mismatch message.
3. The user selects a valid reference document or changes the stocktake warehouse.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow. 

2.14.4 UC 65 - View Stocktake Details
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff 	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Warehouse Keeper or Manager, I want to view a detailed stocktake (inventory audit) sheet, showing physical counted quantities vs. book system quantities, discrepancy values, and notes, so that I can audit inventory losses/gains and authorize adjustments. 
Preconditions	1. The user must be logged in with inventory view permissions.
2. The user must navigate to the Stocktake list page (/stocktakes). 
Postconditions	The system renders the detailed Stocktake view pane below the table (aligned with the master-detail pattern in ExportSlipPage.jsx), displaying document meta-data and line discrepancy details. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1.	The user clicks a stocktake code link (e.g. PKK-2026-0001) or the eye icon (icon bi-eye) on a row in the stocktake table.
2.	The system triggers a detail section slide/reveal below the main table (detailSection).
3.	The system makes a request to the backend: GET /api/v1/stocktakes/{id}.
4.	The backend queries the STOCKTAKES and child STOCKTAKE_LINES tables, joins products/variants/warehouses info, and returns a 200 OK response.
5.	The system renders the Stocktake metadata header:
●	Số chứng từ (Stocktake Code), Kho hàng (Warehouse Name), Ngày kiểm kê (Stocktake Date)
●	Trạng thái (Status Badge: Nháp, Chờ duyệt, Đã duyệt, Đã hủy)
●	Ghi chú (General Note), Nhân viên lập (Created By), Người duyệt (Approved By)
6.	The system renders the items grid (detailTable) displaying:
●	Mã hàng (Variant SKU)
●	Tên hàng (Variant Name)
●	Đơn vị tính (Unit)
●	Số lượng sổ sách (System Qty)
●	Số lượng thực tế (Counted Qty)
●	Chênh lệch (Difference Qty = Counted - System)
●	Đơn giá vốn (Unit Cost)
●	Giá trị chênh lệch (Difference Value = Difference * Unit Cost, formatted with color: Green for positive surplus, Red for negative deficit)
●	Ghi chú dòng (Line Note)
7.	The footer displays the totals:
●	Tổng thực tế (Total Counted Items sum)
●	Tổng giá trị chênh lệch (Total Discrepancy Value)
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Alternative Flow 1: Approve Stocktake document
1.	If the document status is SUBMITTED (Chờ duyệt) and the user has a MANAGER role, the "Duyệt phiếu" (Approve Slip) button is visible in the details footer.
2.	The Manager clicks the "Duyệt phiếu" button.
3.	The system prompts a confirmation modal: "Xác nhận duyệt và ghi sổ phiếu kiểm kho?"
4.	The Manager clicks "Xác nhận". The system makes a backend request: PUT /api/v1/stocktakes/{id}/approve.
5.	The backend corrects inventory ledger records, adjusts warehouse balances, updates document status to POSTED, and returns success. The system updates the UI status badge to Đã duyệt/Đã ghi sổ.
Alternative Flow 2: Print Stocktake Slip
1.	The user clicks the "In phiếu" (Print Slip) button in the details footer.
2.	The system opens a clean print-preview overlay rendering the stocktake sheet formatting, and calls the native print dialog (window.print()).
Alternative Flow 3: Cancel Stocktake
1.	If the document is DRAFT or SUBMITTED, the user clicks the "Hủy phiếu" (Cancel) button.
2.	The system prompts confirmation and updates status to CANCELLED (Đã hủy) without applying stock adjustments.

2.14.5 UC 66 - Update Stocktake
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	None
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to update stocktake so that incorrect or changed stocktake management information can be corrected while preserving traceability.

- Related business data is revalidated when applicable.

6. The system displays the Stocktake update form with current data pre-filled.
7. The user modifies the needed information.
8. The user clicks Save.
9. The system validates mandatory fields, data format, uniqueness rules, and business constraints.
10. The system saves the updated stocktake information.
11. The system records the update action in the Activity Log.
12. The system displays a success message and returns the user to the detail screen or list screen. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.

Step 3.1 - Record not found
1. The search condition does not match any record or the selected record no longer exists.
2. The system displays "Record not found".
3. The user returns to the list and searches again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Record is locked or not editable
1. The system checks the record status.
2. The record is already posted, cancelled, deleted, inactive, or linked to a locked transaction.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a message explaining why the record cannot be updated.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Invalid or missing updated data

2. The system displays validation errors.
3. The user corrects the data.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Duplicate business key
1. The updated value duplicates an existing code, name, email, phone number, serial number, or document number.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a duplicate-value message.
3. The user changes the duplicated value.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Concurrent update conflict
1. Another user updates the same record before the current user saves.
2. The system detects the data version conflict.
3. The system asks the user to reload the latest data before saving again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.2 - Stocktake is posted or cancelled
1. The system checks the stocktake status.
2. The stocktake is Posted or Cancelled.
3. The system blocks direct editing and displays a status-related message.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.3 - Invalid counted quantity or duplicated line
1. The user updates counted quantity or line items.
2. The system detects negative quantity, invalid number, or duplicate item/serial/lot.
3. The system rejects the save action and asks the user to correct the data.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.

4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 3.1 - No matching record
1. The search condition does not match any record.
2. The system displays "No data found".
3. The user changes the search condition.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Record not found or inactive
1. The selected record no longer exists, has been deleted, or has become inactive.
2. The system displays a message such as "Record not found or no longer available".
3. The system returns the user to the list screen.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow. 

2.16.3 UC 74 - Create Repair Ticket
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	Mail Service (optional notification) 
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to create repair ticket so that new warranty management information can be recorded and tracked in the system. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Warranty Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- A new repair ticket record is created successfully.
- The creation action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related inventory, serial, warranty, or report data is updated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to create repair tickets.
3. The user navigates to the Warranty/Repair module.
4. The user clicks Create Repair Ticket.
5. The system displays the repair ticket form.
6. The user searches for customer/product by phone number, warranty code, serial number, or sales order.
7. The system retrieves related warranty and product information if available.
8. The user enters issue description, received date, repair type, warranty eligibility result, and notes.
9. The user clicks Save or Submit.
10. The system validates mandatory fields, warranty status, serial number, customer information, and received date.
11. The system saves the repair ticket and sets the initial repair status.
12. The system updates serial status to Warranty Hold or Repairing if applicable.
13. The system sends notification to the customer if email notification is enabled.
14. The system records the creation action in the Activity Log.
15. The system displays a success message. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 6.1 - Product or customer cannot be found
1. The search condition does not match any warranty, serial number, sales order, or customer.
2. The system displays "No matching warranty/product found".
3. The user corrects the search condition or creates a non-warranty repair ticket if permitted.
Return to step 6 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - Missing issue description or received date
1. The user leaves issue description, received date, or other required information blank.
2. The system displays required-field validation messages.
3. The user enters the missing information.
Return to step 8 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 10.1 - Warranty expired or voided
1. The selected product exists but warranty is expired, voided, or cancelled.
2. The system marks the ticket as non-warranty repair or asks the user to confirm paid repair handling.
3. The user confirms the correct repair type.
Return to step 8 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 12.1 - Serial status cannot be updated
1. The system cannot update the serial number status due to invalid current status or service error.
2. The system rolls back the transaction and displays an error message.
3. The user retries after the issue is resolved.
Return to step 9 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 13.1 - Notification sending fails
1. The repair ticket is saved, but the Mail Service cannot send notification.
2. The system displays a warning while keeping the repair ticket created.
3. The user may resend notification later.

2.16.5 UC 76- Update Repair Ticket
Primary Actors
Manager, Staff with assigned permission	Secondary Actors	Mail Service (optional notification) 
Description	As a Manager, Staff with assigned permission, I want to update repair ticket so that incorrect or changed warranty management information can be corrected while preserving traceability. 
Preconditions	- The user has logged into the system successfully.
- The user has permission to access the Warranty Management feature.
- The backend server and database are functioning normally. 
Postconditions	- The repair ticket information is updated successfully.
- The update action is recorded in the Activity Log.
- Related business data is revalidated when applicable. 
Normal Sequence/Flow	1. The user logs into the system successfully.
2. The system checks whether the user has permission to update repair tickets.
3. The user navigates to the Repair Ticket List screen.
4. The user searches for and opens the repair ticket.
5. The user clicks Edit/Update.
6. The system displays the repair ticket update form with current data pre-filled.
7. The user updates repair status, solution description, repair cost, completed date, appointment date, or note.
8. The user clicks Save.
9. The system validates mandatory fields, date order, status transition, warranty eligibility, and cost rules.
10. The system saves the updated repair ticket.
11. The system updates related warranty/serial status if the repair status changes.
12. The system sends notification to the customer if notification is enabled.
13. The system records the update action in the Activity Log.
14. The system displays a success message. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 4.1 - Repair ticket not found
1. The search condition does not match any repair ticket or the selected ticket no longer exists.
2. The system displays "Repair ticket not found".
3. The user searches again.
Return to step 3 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 5.1 - Ticket is cancelled or completed and locked
1. The system checks the repair ticket status.
2. The ticket is in a locked status that does not allow direct editing.
3. The system blocks editing and displays a status-related message.
Return to step 4 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Invalid status transition
1. The user changes the repair status in a sequence that violates the allowed repair workflow.
2. The system rejects the save action and displays a status-transition validation message.
3. The user selects a valid next status.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.2 - Invalid cost or completed date
1. The repair cost is negative, completed date is earlier than received date, or completed date is missing for a completed repair.
2. The system displays validation messages.
3. The user corrects the invalid information.
Return to step 7 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 12.1 - Notification sending fails
1. The repair ticket update is saved, but the Mail Service cannot send notification.
2. The system displays a warning and allows the user to resend notification later.

2. The system checks whether the user has permission to access reporting functions.
3. The user navigates to the View Reports screen.
4. The system displays available filter conditions such as date range, warehouse, product, partner, status, and document type depending on the report.
5. The user enters or selects filter conditions.
6. The user clicks View/Generate.
7. The system validates the filter conditions.
8. The system retrieves and aggregates data from the relevant modules.
9. The system displays the report in table/chart format with export or print options if available.
10. The user reviews the report result. 
Alternative Sequences/Flows	Step 2.1 - No permission
1. The system checks the user's assigned permissions.
2. The user does not have permission to perform this function.
3. The system blocks the action and displays an "Access denied" message.
4. The system keeps the user on the previous page or redirects the user to the Home page.
 
Step 5.1 - Missing or invalid filter condition
1. The user leaves required filter information blank or enters an invalid date range.
2. The system displays validation messages.
3. The user corrects the filter condition.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.1 - No report data found
1. No data matches the selected filter condition.
2. The system displays "No data found".
3. The user changes the filter condition.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 8.2 - Data range is too large
1. The selected filter range is too broad and may affect performance.
2. The system asks the user to narrow the range or confirms long-running report generation.
3. The user narrows the filter or confirms the action.
Return to step 5 of Normal Flow.
 
Step 9.1 - Export or print fails
1. The user uses export/print option but the file generation or printer service fails.
2. The system displays an error message while keeping the report result visible.
3. The user retries export/print later.

3. Functional Requirements
3.1 Authentication 
3.1.1 Login 
3.1.1.1 Login Screen Interface 
 
This screen allows the Super Admin, Manager, or Staff to:
●	Enter Credentials: Input the registered email address or unique username and password.
●	Toggle Password Visibility: Click the inline eye icon to switch between masked and plain text formats.
●	Remember Session: Check the remember me checkbox to persist the active authentication state across browser sessions.
●	Execute Login: Click the login button to submit credentials and access the application dashboard.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	Trigger Google SSO: Click the login with Google button to initiate federated identity verification.
●	Navigate to Recovery: Click the forgot password link to open the multi-step password recovery wizard.
Field Name	Description
Form Title 	Display text: "Đăng nhập" (Sign In). 
Subtitle 	Display text: "Chào mừng trở lại! Vui lòng nhập thông tin của bạn." 
Email hoặc Tên đăng nhập 	Data Type: String
Min/Max Length: 5 - 100 characters
Format: Email format or unique alphanumeric string
Initial Data: Empty
Mật khẩu 	Data Type: String
Min/Max Length: 8 - 50 characters
Format: Masked text characters (••••••)
Initial Data: Empty
UI Component: Includes an inline "Eye" icon to toggle password visibility (Show/Hide).
Ghi nhớ đăng nhập 	Data Type: Boolean (Checkbox)
Initial Data: Unchecked
Description: Persists the active user session across browser tabs and system restarts.
Quên mật khẩu? 	Data Type: Hyperlink
Description: Redirects the user to the Forgot Password multi-step wizard workflow (UC-FE-01-04).

Đăng nhập (Button) 	Data Type: Action Button
Description: Validates client inputs against the system identity database and logs the actor into the system based on assigned permissions. Features an integrated login icon.
hoặc tiếp tục với 	Data Type: Text Divider
Description: Visually separates standard credential authorization from external Federated Single Sign-On (SSO).

Đăng nhập bằng Google (Button) 	Data Type: Action Button
Description: Triggers the Google OAuth2 external protocol to initiate federated identity verification. Displays the standard Google logo.
Footer Links 	Secondary navigation links for user assistance: Hỗ trợ (Support), Bảo mật (Security), and Điều khoản (Terms). 
Copyright Text 	Baseline validation text: © 2026 DUY LONG COMPUTER SYSTEM. ALL RIGHTS RESERVED. 

3.1.2 Login with Google
3.1.2.1 Google Account Selection Screen 
 
This screen allows the Super Admin, Manager, or Staff to:
●	Select Google Account: Choose an existing, pre-authenticated corporate Google account from the displayed session cache.
●	Add Alternative Account: Select the option to use another account to manually input unlisted corporate Google credentials.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	Change Interface Language: Click the localized language dropdown menu to modify the system interface rendering layout.
●	Review Legal Disclaimers: Click the integrated hyperlinks to inspect the privacy policies or terms of service agreements.


Field Name

Modal Prompt Header 	Alert title notification banner text display: "Xác nhận xóa nhà cung cấp". 
Warning Description Block 	Text container prompting final verification before removal operations: "Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa nhà cung cấp Công ty TNHH Intel Việt Nam (NCC001)?". 
System Destructive Alert 	Critical operational notice emphasized inside a light red status container row: "Hành động này không thể hoàn tác.". 
Hủy (Button) 	Safe selection text border link layout button designed to dismiss modal masks and safeguard system properties. 
Xác nhận xóa (Button) 	High-contrast red block execution interface tool that deletes the supplier profile across database lookup streams. 

3.6 Product Management
3.6.1 View Product List
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	View inventory overview: quickly monitor high-level metrics through summary cards, including the total number of items, items running low on stock (Sắp hết hàng), and out-of-stock items (Hết hàng).
●	Manage goods and services list: view a comprehensive catalog of all items, showing details such as item code, name, type, tax status, current stock quantity, and total inventory value.
●	Search and filter items: locate specific items by entering keywords in the search bar, or use the advanced filter (Lọc nâng cao) to narrow down the list based on specific criteria.
●	Export data: download the current list of items into a spreadsheet format by clicking Xuất Excel.
●	Create new items: add a new product or service to the system catalog by clicking the + Thêm mới button.
●	Execute row-level actions: quickly modify an item's details using the edit icon (pencil) or access additional actions via the more options icon (three vertical dots) in the Chức năng column.
●	Monitor insights: view visual analytics, such as the 30-day inbound inventory trend chart (Xu hướng nhập kho) located at the bottom of the dashboard.
Field Name
Description
Tổng danh mục hàng hóa 	Summary card displaying the total count of active products/items registered in the system. 
Sắp hết hàng 	Summary card highlighting the number of items that have dropped to or below their low-stock warning threshold. 
Hết hàng 	Summary card highlighting the number of items that have dropped to or below their low-stock warning threshold. 
Tìm theo tên hoặc mã hàng... 	Global search bar for the grid, allowing users to type and find items by their specific code or name. 
Lọc nâng cao 	Button used to open a panel with additional, complex filtering options. 
Xuất Excel 	Button used to export the currently displayed table data into an Excel (.xlsx) file. 
+ Thêm mới 	Button used to open the creation form for a new good or service. 
Ô chọn dòng (Checkbox) 	Allows the user to select one or more items in the list for bulk actions. 
Mã hàng 	Displays the unique alphanumeric identifier for the item (e.g., VT00001). 
Tên hàng hóa, dịch vụ 	Displays the full name of the product or service, accompanied by a categorized icon. 
Tính chất 	Indicates the classification of the record using a label, such as HÀNG HÓA (Goods) or DỊCH VỤ (Service). 
Giảm thuế 	Displays the VAT or tax reduction status applicable to the item (e.g., 8% (VAT) or Chưa xác định). 
Số lượng tồn 	Shows the current physical quantity available in the warehouse. Negative or critical values are highlighted in red (e.g., (1,00)). 
Giá trị tồn 	Calculates and displays the total financial value of the current stock for that specific item line. 
Chức năng 	Contains quick-action icons for each row: an edit button (pencil) to modify the item, and a menu button (three dots) for more options. 
Tổng cộng (Footer) 	Displays the aggregate sums for the Số lượng tồn (Total Stock) and Giá trị tồn (Total Value) columns across the current view. 

3.6.2 Item Type Selection Modal
 
his modal screen appears when the Manager / Staff clicks the + Thêm mới button on the main Goods and Services management page. It allows them to:
●	Select item nature: choose the specific classification of the new item they want to create (e.g., Goods, Materials, Finished Products, or Services). This step ensures the system routes the user to the correct data entry form with appropriate accounting and inventory rules.
●	Cancel action: abort the creation process and return to the main list by closing the modal.
Field Name
Description
Tiêu đề 	Displays "Chọn tính chất hàng hóa dịch vụ", guiding the user to select the correct category before proceeding. 
Nút Đóng (X icon) 	Action button located at the top right, used to close the modal window without making a selection. 
Hàng hóa 	Selection button to create standard goods. Includes a sub-description indicating these are products bought and resold to customers (Sản phẩm bạn mua và bán lại cho khách hàng). 
Vật tư & Phụ tùng 	Selection button to create materials or spare parts. Includes a sub-description indicating these are input materials used for production, construction, or providing services (Nguyên liệu đầu vào dùng cho hoạt động sản xuất, xây dựng, cung cấp dịch vụ). 
Thành phẩm 	Selection button to create finished products. Includes a sub-description indicating these are output products resulting from the manufacturing process (Là sản phẩm đầu ra của quá trình sản xuất). 
Dịch vụ 	Selection button to create service items. Includes a sub-description indicating these are intangible services provided to customers (Dịch vụ mà bạn cung cấp cho khách hàng). 

3.6.3 Create Product
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	Input core item details: enter mandatory information such as the item name and code, and select its classification group and primary unit of measurement.
●	Configure tax and warranty: set applicable tax reduction rules and define the product's warranty period.
●	Set inventory thresholds: define the minimum stock quantity to trigger low-stock alerts in the system.
●	Add supplementary descriptions: input the item's origin, a general description, and specific notes tailored for buying and selling transactions.
●	Upload visual assets: upload a product image (up to 2MB, JPG/PNG) to visually identify the item within the system.
●	Change item nature: click the Thay đổi tính chất link at the top to switch the item classification (e.g., from Goods to Service) if initially selected incorrectly.
●	Execute save actions: choose to Cancel (Hủy), Save and close (Cất), or Save and immediately open a blank form to add another item (Cất và Thêm).
Field Name
Description
Tên (*) 	Required text input field to enter the full name of the material, good, or service. 
Mã (*) 	Required text input field for the unique item identifier (e.g., VT00003). Often auto-generated by the system based on a sequence but can be manually modified. 
Nhóm VTHH 	Dropdown and search field to assign the item to a specific category/group. Includes a + button to quickly create a new group without leaving the form. 
Đơn vị tính chính 	Dropdown list to select the primary unit of measurement (e.g., piece, box, kg). Includes a + button to quickly add a new unit. 
Giảm thuế theo quy định 	Dropdown list to apply specific tax reduction policies to the item. Includes a Tra cứu giảm thuế link to quickly look up applicable tax regulations. 
Tải ảnh lên 	Image upload area supporting click or drag-and-drop. Allows a maximum file size of 2MB (JPG, PNG). Includes edit (pencil) and delete (trash bin) actions below it once an image is uploaded. 
Thời hạn bảo hành 	Consists of a numeric input field and a unit dropdown (e.g., Tháng - Month) to define the duration the item is covered under warranty. 
Số lượng tồn tối thiểu 	Numeric input field used to set the reorder point or low-stock warning threshold for inventory management purposes. 
Nguồn gốc 	Text input field to specify the manufacturing origin, brand, or source of the product. 
Mô tả 	Multi-line text area to provide comprehensive details, features, or specifications about the item. 
Diễn giải khi mua 	Text input field for specific notes or default descriptions that will automatically populate on purchasing documents. 
Diễn giải khi bán 	Text input field for specific notes or default descriptions that will automatically populate on sales documents. 
Hủy 	Button used to discard any inputted data, cancel the creation/edit process, and close the panel. 
Cất 	Button used to save the item data into the system and close the form panel. 
Cất và Thêm 	Button used to save the current item and immediately refresh the panel to a blank state to create another new record, facilitating rapid data entry. 

3.6.4 Print Barcode
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	Select code format: choose whether to generate standard Barcodes (Mã vạch) or QR codes (Mã QR) for the items.
●	Configure paper layout: select the physical paper size and layout format (e.g., 2 stamps, A4 sheet, custom columns) to ensure labels print correctly on the chosen media.
●	Customize label content: toggle specific item information to be printed on the label, such as item code, name, selling price, and company name.
●	Preview label design: view a live visual representation of how the printed label will look based on the selected configuration and templates.
●	Select items for printing: filter and select specific goods from the inventory list that require physical labels.
●	Define print quantities: manually input the exact number of labels needed for each selected item.
●	Execute print job: integrate with an external accounting print driver if needed, and trigger the final printing process.
Field Name
Description
LOẠI TEM IN 	Radio button group allowing the user to select the code type to generate: Mã vạch (1D Barcode) or Mã QR (2D QR Code). 
CHỌN KHỔ GIẤY 	Radio button group providing preset paper dimensions and layout grids (e.g., 2 stamps per row, A4 with 65 stamps). Includes a Tùy chọn (Custom) field to manually define the number of stamps per row. 
THÔNG TIN HIỂN THỊ 	Checkboxes to toggle which details appear on the printed label: Mã hàng (Item Code), Tên hàng (Item Name), Tên công ty (Company Name), and Giá bán (Selling Price). 
MẪU IN & XEM TRƯỚC 	Radio buttons to select predefined label design templates (Mẫu 1, 2, 3) and a Preview box displaying a real-time mock-up of the final label. 
Lọc 	Button used to open advanced filtering options to quickly find specific items to print. 
Ô chọn dòng (Checkbox) 	Allows the user to select which specific items in the grid they want to print labels for. 
Mã hàng 	Displays the unique alphanumeric identifier for the item. 
Tên hàng 	Displays the full name of the product or good. 
Nhóm VTHH 	Displays the category or group the item belongs to (e.g., HH - Hàng hóa). 
Đơn giá bán 	Shows the current selling price of the item, which can be printed on the label if configured. 
Số lượng tồn 	Shows the current physical quantity in stock. 
Số lượng cần in 	Numeric input field where the user specifies exactly how many copies of the label to print for that specific item line. 
Trạng thái 	Visual badge indicating the current inventory status of the item (e.g., Hết hàng - Out of stock, Chờ nhập - Pending inbound, Sẵn sàng - Ready). 
In bằng trình in AMIS Kế toán 	Toggle switch to route the print job through the AMIS Accounting system's print engine instead of the standard browser or local system print dialog. 
Hủy 	Button used to cancel the print configuration process and close the screen. 
In 	Button used to finalize the setup and send the selected labels and quantities to the printer.

●	Export Data: Click the export action button to download the current data grid into a spreadsheet format for offline reporting or analysis.
●	Create New Brands: Click the add action button to initialize the workflow for registering a new manufacturer into the system.
●	Modify Existing Records: Utilize inline action icons within the data grid to edit brand details or securely remove obsolete brands.
●	Track Workspace Context: Use the breadcrumb trail tracking navigation links to understand the current hierarchical position within the system workspace.
Field Name
Description
Top Navigation Bar 	Global dashboard header displaying the primary system brand title (Duy Long Computer), notification alerts, settings utilities, and the active profile box. 
Breadcrumb Menu 	Navigational tracking path: Trang chủ > Quản lý thương hiệu
Form Title 	Display text header at the top left of the workspace: "Nhà sản xuất". 
Search Input Bar 	Data Type: String. Placeholder text: "Nhập tìm kiếm tên nhà sản xuất...". Description: Filters the data grid based on matching queries. Features an inline magnifying glass search icon.
Xuất Excel (Button) 	Data Type: Export Action Button. Description: Generates and downloads a .xlsx file containing the currently displayed or filtered dataset. Features a green Excel logo icon. 
Thêm mới (Button) 	Data Type: Action Trigger Button. Description: Opens the "Create New Manufacturer" form (modal or new page). Features a white '+' icon on a primary blue background. 
Data Grid Component 	Dynamic tabular view displaying manufacturer records. Columns are clearly defined for structured data consumption. 
Mã NSX 	Used heavily as a foreign key in Inventory and Warranty modules. 
Tên nhà sản xuất	The official registered name of the brand (e.g., Dell, HP, Asus). 
Mô tả 	Optional context, details, or notes regarding the manufacturer. 
Người tạo 	Reference to the user profile that initially created the database record 
Ngày tạo 	Timestamp indicating the exact date the record was inserted into the database.

Pagination Footer 	Tracking text: "Hiển thị [X] - [Y] trên tổng [Z]". Includes a dropdown to adjust records per page (default: 10/page) and standard pagination navigation controls (Previous, 1, 2, 3..., Next).

This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Input Manufacturer Details: Enter critical information to register a new brand into the system, including its unique code, official name, and descriptive notes.
●	Apply Data Updates: Click the save action button to validate the inputs and commit the new manufacturer record to the system database.
●	Cancel Operation: Click the cancel button to discard all entered data, clear the form, and return safely to the primary Manufacturer Management data grid without making any changes.
Field Name
Description
Form Title / Modal Header 	Display text: "Thêm mới nhà sản xuất" (Add New Manufacturer) or "Cập nhật thông tin" (Update Manufacturer) depending on the active user trigger. 
Mã NSX 	Unique identifier for the brand. Can be configured to auto-generate incrementally (e.g., NSX0003) or allow manual input. 
Business Rule: Must be validated for absolute uniqueness against existing database records to prevent duplication.
Tên nhà sản xuất	The official registered name of the manufacturer (e.g., Asus, Logitech). 
Exception Case: If left blank upon submission, trigger validation message: "Tên nhà sản xuất không được để trống".
Mô tả	Additional context, notes, internal guidelines, or contact details related to this specific manufacturer.
Lưu (Button)	If validation passes, it inserts (or updates) the record in the database, closes the form, and refreshes the parent data grid. Usually accompanied by a primary color styling
Hủy bỏ (Button)	Discards all user inputs, bypasses all validation rules, closes the form/modal, and returns the user to the previous workspace view.

3.7.4 Update Brand
 This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	View Pre-loaded Data: The system automatically retrieves and populates the form with the current details of the selected manufacturer from the database.
●	Modify Brand Information: Update permissible fields such as the brand name or the detailed description.
●	Apply Updates: Click the save action button to trigger input validation and overwrite the existing record in the system database with the new values.
●	Cancel Operation: Click the cancel button to discard all modifications, preserve the original record state, and return safely to the manufacturer data grid.
Field Name
Description
Breadcrumb Menu 	Navigational tracking path: Trang chủ > Quản lý thương hiệu > Chỉnh sửa
Form Title 	Display text header: "Chỉnh sửa thương hiệu”
Mã NSX 	Unique identifier for the brand. 
Business Rule: Modification is strictly prohibited to maintain data integrity and prevent breaking Foreign Key relationships with the Inventory and Warranty modules. 
Tên thương hiệu	The official registered name of the manufacturer (e.g., Asus, Dell). 
Exception Case: If left blank upon submission, trigger validation message: "Tên thương hiệu không được để trống". 
Mô tả 	Additional context, notes, or details related to this specific manufacturer. 
Lưu (Button) 	Upon successful validation, updates the database record, displays a success toast message ("Cập nhật thông tin thương hiệu thành công"), and automatically redirects the user back to the Brand Management list. 
Hủy bỏ (Button) 	Discards all unsubmitted text modifications, closes the update interface without altering the database, and redirects user focus back to the parent data grid layout. 
3.7.5 Delete Brand 
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Review Deletion Target: Clearly identify which manufacturer record is staged for deletion via the dynamic confirmation message.
●	Confirm Destructive Action: Click the confirm action button to execute the removal of the selected brand from the database.
●	Cancel Operation: Click the cancel button to safely dismiss the warning prompt and return to the data grid without altering any system data.
Field Name
Description
Modal Title 	Display text header: "Xác nhận xóa Thương hiệu"
Warning Icon 	A prominent caution icon (typically an exclamation mark inside a triangle) to visually alert the user that they are about to perform a potentially destructive and irreversible action.
Confirmation Prompt	The system dynamically inserts the selected brand's name into the string to ensure the user knows exactly which record is being targeted.
Xác nhận (Button)	Triggers the backend API to delete the record.
Business Rule (Referential Integrity Check): Before actual deletion, the system MUST verify if this Mã NSX (Brand Code) is currently linked to any active Products, Inventory Logs, or Warranty Tickets.
* Happy Path: If no links exist, the record is deleted (or soft-deleted via a status change), the modal closes, the grid refreshes, and a success toast appears ("Xóa nhà sản xuất thành công").
* Exception Case: If the brand is currently in use, the deletion must be blocked, and an error message must be displayed: "Không thể xóa nhà sản xuất này vì đang có dữ liệu sản phẩm/bảo hành liên quan." (Cannot delete this manufacturer as it is linked to existing product/warranty data).
Hủy bỏ (Button)	Safely aborts the deletion process, closes the modal dialog immediately, and returns the user's focus to the underlying Brand Management data grid.

Field Name
Description
Form Title / Modal Header	Display text: "Cập nhật loại sản phẩm"

Tên loại SP	Data Type: String. State: Mandatory. Initial Data: Pre-filled with the current category name (e.g., Màn hình máy tính). Description: Allows the user to modify the official registered name of the category.
Exception Case: If the user clears the field and attempts to submit, the system must block the action and trigger the validation message: "Tên loại sản phẩm không được để trống" (Category name cannot be blank). 
Mô tả	Data Type: String (Long Text). State: Optional. Format: Multi-line text area (Textarea). Initial Data: Pre-filled with existing notes (if any). Description: Allows updating, correcting, or appending additional context and general characteristics regarding the product category.
Hủy bỏ	Data Type: Cancel Action Button. Description: Discards all unsubmitted text modifications, closes the update modal/form without altering the database, and redirects user focus back to the parent data grid.
Lưu	Data Type: Primary Action Button. Description: Transmits modified data for backend verification. Upon successful validation, it updates the database record, displays a success toast message ("Cập nhật loại sản phẩm thành công"), closes the modal, and automatically refreshes the Category Management list.
3.8.5 Delete Category 
 
This modal dialog allows the Manager, Staff to:

Field Name
Description
Modal Title 	Display text header: "Xóa loại sản phẩm"

Confirmation Prompt	The system dynamically inserts the selected category's name into the string to ensure the user knows exactly which record is being targeted.
Xác nhận	Data Type: Destructive Action Button. Description: Triggers the backend API to delete the record.



Critical Business Rule (Referential Integrity Check): Before actual deletion, the system MUST verify if this Mã loại SP (Category Code) is currently linked to any active Products in the inventory.
* Happy Path: If no links exist, the record is deleted (or soft-deleted), the modal closes, the grid refreshes, and a success toast appears ("Xóa loại sản phẩm thành công").
* Exception Case: If the category is currently in use by one or more products, the deletion must be blocked, and an error message must be displayed: "Không thể xóa loại sản phẩm này vì đang có hàng hóa liên kết." (Cannot delete this category as it is linked to existing products).
Hủy bỏ	Data Type: Cancel Action Button. Description: Safely aborts the deletion process, closes the modal dialog immediately, and returns the user's focus to the underlying Product Category data grid.

3.9 Unit Management
3.9.1 View Unit List
 

This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	View unit list: view the complete list of measurement units used throughout the warehouse management system.
●	Search units: use the search box to quickly find a unit by its name.
●	Create new unit: create a new measurement unit by clicking the Thêm button.
●	Update unit information: modify the name or description of an existing unit by clicking the Sửa button.
●	Change unit status: activate or deactivate a unit when it is no longer used.
●	Refresh data: reload the latest unit information from the system.
●	Export data: export the current unit list to an Excel file.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View unit information including unit name, description, and current status.
●	Identify active and inactive units through the status column.
●	Access additional actions through the action menu in the Chức năng column.
●	Navigate through pages using the pagination controls at the bottom of the screen.
Field Name	Description
Search Unit	Text box used to search measurement units by unit name.
Refresh	Button used to reload the latest unit data from the system.
Export Excel	Button used to export the current unit list to an Excel file.
Thêm	Button used to create a new measurement unit.
Đơn vị tính	Displays the name of the measurement unit (e.g., Cái, Bộ, Hộp, Thùng).
Mô tả	Displays additional information or notes about the unit.
Trạng thái	Displays the current status of the unit (Đang sử dụng / Ngừng sử dụng).
Sửa	Button used to edit the selected unit information.
Action Menu	Dropdown menu containing additional actions such as Activate or Deactivate.
Pagination	Allows the user to move between pages and control the number of displayed records.
Total Records	Displays the total number of unit records currently available in the system.

3.10 Inventory Import Management
3.10.1 View Import History
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	View import receipt list: view the comprehensive list of warehouse import vouchers, tracking incoming goods from suppliers.
●	Filter the list: filter the records by a specific date range (Từ ngày, Đến ngày) and the current processing status (Tình trạng).
●	Search vouchers: use the top search bar to quickly find specific receipts by keyword or voucher code.
●	Create new inbound voucher: initiate the creation of a new import receipt by clicking the + Thêm mới button (or the + Tạo đơn mới button in the sidebar).
●	Reset filters: quickly clear all current filter criteria to view the entire list by clicking Làm mới.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View voucher details: click the eye icon in the Thao tác column or click the hyperlinked voucher number to review the specific details of an inbound transaction.
●	Review processing status: easily identify the state of each receipt using visual status badges such as Hoàn thành (Completed), Chờ duyệt (Pending Approval), and Đã hủy (Cancelled).
●	Navigate through pages: move between result pages using the pagination control at the bottom right.
Field Name
Description
Từ ngày	Initial value is the start date of the filtering period. The user selects a date to filter inbound receipts recorded from this point onward. 
Đến ngày	Initial value is the end date of the filtering period. The user selects a date to filter inbound receipts recorded up to this point. 
Tình trạng (Filter) 	Dropdown list allowing the user to filter the grid by specific statuses (e.g., Tất cả - All, Completed, Pending, Cancelled). 
Làm mới 	Button used to reset all filter fields back to their default, empty states. 
Lọc dữ liệu 	Button used to apply the selected date and status filters and update the data grid accordingly. 
+ Thêm mới / + Tạo đơn mới 	Button used to navigate to the form to create a brand new inbound warehouse receipt. 
Ngày ghi nhận 	Displays the recorded or posting date of the inbound transaction. 
Số phiếu 	Displays the unique voucher code of the receipt (e.g., PNK-2405-001). Clicking the blue hyperlink opens the full voucher details. 
Đối tác 	Displays the name of the supplier, vendor, or partner providing the incoming goods. 
Kho hàng 	Indicates the specific destination warehouse or storage location receiving the inventory. 
Tổng tiền 	Displays the total monetary value of the goods listed on the inbound receipt. 
Tình trạng (Column) 	Displays the current approval or processing status of the receipt using a color-coded label. 
Thao tác 	Contains the action icon (an eye) to view the full details of the specific inbound voucher. 
3.10.2 Create Import Slip 
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	Input general supplier information: select or enter the supplier's code, name, and address, assign the responsible staff member, and provide a reason for the transaction.
●	Set voucher details: specify the recording date, view the auto-generated voucher number, link related documents (e.g., Purchase/Sales Orders), and attach supporting files.
●	Manage item list: populate the receipt by adding items manually line-by-line (Thêm dòng mới), quick-adding from the catalog (Thêm nhanh từ danh mục), or bulk importing via a spreadsheet (Nhập từ Excel).
●	Manage Serial Numbers: specifically track and input individual serial numbers for items that require strict tracking using the Quản lý Serial feature.
●	Review financial totals: automatically calculate and review the total quantity and the total financial value of the inbound shipment.
●	Execute bottom-bar actions: choose to cancel (Hủy bỏ), view the edit history (Xem nhật ký thay đổi), save as draft (Lưu tạm), save/commit the voucher (Lưu lại), or save and print (Lưu và In).
Field Name
Description
Mã nhà cung cấp 	Input field to enter or search for the supplier's code. Includes a + button to quickly create a new supplier record without leaving the page. 
Tên nhà cung cấp 	Text field displaying the full name of the selected supplier. 
Địa chỉ 	Text field displaying the supplier's address. 
Nhân viên bán hàng 	Dropdown/search field to select the staff member assigned to this transaction. Includes a + button to add a new staff member. (Note: The UI label says "bán hàng" (sales), but in this context, it represents the responsible staff). 
Lý do xuất 	Text area to input the specific reason, contract reference, or description for the transaction. (Note: The UI label says "xuất" (export), but it functions as the general description field for this receipt). 
Ngày ghi nhận 	Date picker to set the posting or recording date of the inbound voucher. 
Số phiếu 	Displays the system-generated voucher code for this receipt (e.g., PNK-20231027-004). 
Liên kết liên quan 	Hyperlink area to view or attach references to predecessor documents, such as an Order (Đơn hàng: SO-2023-1022). 
Đính kèm chứng từ... 	Area to upload and manage attached files, such as scanned invoices or delivery notes. 
Nhập từ Excel 	Button used to bulk import item lines into the table using a pre-formatted Excel template. 
Mã hàng / Tên hàng 	Displays the unique code and full name of the item being received. 
Kho 	Dropdown list to select the specific destination warehouse for each item line. Includes a + button to quickly add a new warehouse location. 
Serial Number 	Displays the serial number input status (e.g., 5/5 SNs inputted or Thiếu 7 Serial missing). Includes a Quản lý Serial link to open a modal for entering or scanning individual item serials. 
ĐVT 	Displays the unit of measurement for the item (e.g., Cái, Bộ). 
Bảo hành 	Displays the warranty duration applicable to the item (e.g., 36 tháng). 
Số lượng 	Numeric input field to specify the exact quantity of items being received into the warehouse. 
Đơn giá nhập 	Numeric input field to enter the unit cost or purchase price of the incoming item. 
Thành tiền 	Automatically calculated field displaying the total monetary amount for that specific item line (Quantity x Unit Price). 
Icon Xóa (Red Trash Can) 	Action button located at the end of each row to remove that specific item from the voucher. 
+ Thêm dòng mới 	Button used to add a single blank row to the bottom of the table for manual entry. 
Thêm nhanh từ danh mục 	Button used to open a pop-up catalog, allowing the user to select multiple items quickly. 
Tổng cộng hàng nhập 	Footer section of the table aggregating the total quantity (15) and total financial value (124.500.000) of all items listed. 
Hủy bỏ 	Button used to discard all unsaved changes and close the inbound receipt screen. 
Xem nhật ký thay đổi 	Link used to open the audit trail, showing the history of modifications made to this specific voucher. 
Lưu tạm 	Button used to save the current progress as a draft, allowing the user to return and complete it later without affecting actual inventory. 
Lưu lại	Button used to save and commit the inbound voucher to the system, officially updating the inventory. 
Lưu và In 	Button used to save the voucher and immediately trigger the print dialog for physical documentation.

This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:

Lọc dữ liệu 	Button used to apply the selected date and status filters and update the data grid accordingly.

Ngày ghi nhận 	Displays the recorded or posting date of the inbound transaction.

Người nhận 	Displays the name of the staff member responsible for receiving the shipment.
