Tổng cộng hàng nhập (Footer) 	Displays the aggregate sum of the physical Số lượng (Quantity) and the financial Tổng tiền (Total Amount) for all items in this specific receipt. 
3.10.4 Update Import Slip
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to:
●	Edit general information: update existing supplier details, reassign the responsible staff member, or modify the reason for the transaction.
●	Update voucher details: change the recording date, manage related document links (e.g., modifying the linked Purchase Order), or add/remove attached files.
●	Modify item list: add new item lines to the receipt (Thêm dòng mới / Thêm nhanh từ danh mục), bulk add via spreadsheet (Nhập từ Excel), edit specific details of existing rows (such as destination warehouse, quantity, or import price), or remove items using the delete action (red trash icon).
●	Manage Serial Numbers: update, add, or correct individual serial numbers for tracked items using the Quản lý Serial feature to resolve discrepancies (e.g., fixing a "Thiếu 7 Serial" warning).
●	Review recalculated totals: automatically calculate and review the updated total quantity and financial value after any modifications to the item list.
●	Execute bottom-bar actions: choose to discard unsaved changes (Hủy bỏ), view the edit history to see previous versions (Xem nhật ký thay đổi), save modifications as a draft (Lưu tạm), commit the final changes (Lưu lại), or save and print (Lưu và In).
Field Name
Description
Mã nhà cung cấp 	Displays the current supplier's code. Allows the user to clear and select a different supplier if initially entered incorrectly. 
Tên nhà cung cấp / Địa chỉ 	Displays the current supplier's full name and address. Auto-updates if a new supplier code is selected. 
Nhân viên bán hàng 	Dropdown/search field displaying the currently assigned staff member. Allows reassignment to another user. 
Lý do xuất 	Text area displaying the current explanation. The user can modify this text to provide better context or correct typos. 
Ngày ghi nhận 	Date picker displaying the recorded date. The user can adjust this date if the actual posting date needs correction. 
Số phiếu 	Displays the voucher code (e.g., PNK-20231027-004). This is typically a read-only field in edit mode to maintain system tracking integrity. 
Liên kết liên quan 	Hyperlink area to view, add, or remove references to predecessor documents. 
Đính kèm chứng từ... 	Area to upload new files or remove previously attached documents. 
Nhập từ Excel 	Button used to bulk import additional item lines into the table using an Excel template. 
Mã hàng / Tên hàng 	Displays the unique code and full name of the existing items on the receipt. 
Kho 	Dropdown list allowing the user to change the specific destination warehouse for an existing item line. 
Serial Number 	Displays the current serial number input status. Includes a Quản lý Serial link to open a modal for editing, adding, or removing serials to match the physical quantity. 
ĐVT / Bảo hành 	Displays the unit of measurement and warranty duration (typically read-only attributes of the item profile). 
Số lượng 	Editable numeric input field to correct the quantity of items actually received. 
Đơn giá nhập 	Editable numeric input field to correct the unit cost or purchase price. 
Thành tiền 	Automatically recalculated field displaying the total monetary amount for the line based on updated Quantity x Unit Price. 
Icon Xóa (Red Trash Can) 	Action button located at the end of each row to remove that specific item from the receipt. 
Thêm dòng mới / Thêm nhanh... 	Buttons used to add new items to the receipt manually or via a quick-select catalog. 
Tổng cộng hàng nhập 	Footer section dynamically recalculating and displaying the aggregate total quantity and financial value of all items currently in the table. 
Hủy bỏ 	Button used to discard all modifications made during this session and close the edit screen, keeping the original data intact. 
Xem nhật ký thay đổi 	Link used to open the audit trail, displaying who made changes to this voucher in the past and what was altered. 
Lưu tạm 	Button used to save the current modifications as a draft without fully committing them to the permanent inventory ledger. 
Lưu lại 	Button used to save and permanently commit the changes to the system, updating inventory levels accordingly. 
Lưu và In 	Button used to save the modifications and immediately trigger the print preview or printing process.

Field Name
Description

Hủy 	Button used to discard any unsaved modifications and close the screen. 
Lưu tạm 	Button used to save the current modifications as a draft without finalizing. 
Lưu lại 	Button used to save and commit the changes made to the voucher. 
Lưu và In 	Button used to save the modifications and immediately trigger the print preview or printing process. 


3.12 Stock Transfer Management
3.12.1 View Transfer History List
  
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Monitor Inventory Movements: View a comprehensive, paginated grid of all stock transfer tickets (phiếu chuyển kho) between different company branches or warehouses.
●	Filter Data: Utilize multi-criteria dropdown filters (Timeframe, Status, and Location) to quickly isolate specific transfer records.
●	Manage Transfer Workflows: Initiate a new transfer ticket or import existing data batches via Excel.
●	Generate Financial Reports: Access quick reporting tools at the bottom of the screen to download automated PDF summaries for accounting purposes.
Field Name
Description
Global Sidebar Navigation	Left-hand menu containing system modules: Quy trình, Biểu đồ, Nhập kho, Xuất kho, Chuyển kho (Active), Bảng điều khiển, Đơn hàng, etc.
Top Search Bar	Global search input for quickly locating specific ticket IDs or items across the system.
Tổng phiếu	A high-level overview metric showing the total number of transfer tickets based on the currently applied filters.
Nhập từ Excel (Button)	Opens a dialog to upload a .xlsx or .csv file for mass-creating transfer tickets into the system.
Thêm mới (Button)	Redirects the user to the "Create Transfer Ticket" form. Features a primary color styling.
Data Grid Component	Dynamic tabular view displaying warehouse transfer records.
NGÀY GHI NHẬN	The exact date the transfer ticket was created
SỐ PHIẾU	The unique system-generated identifier for the transfer
KHO XUẤT	The origin warehouse from which the stock is being deducted
KHO NHẬP	The receiving warehouse where the stock is being added
TRẠNG THÁI	Indicates the current phase of the transfer. Values include:
• Hoàn thành (Completed): Goods successfully received.
• Đang chuyển (Transferring): Goods in transit.
• Nháp (Draft): Awaiting manager change
Tải báo cáo nhanh	Contains automated accounting summaries. Subtext: "Tự động tổng hợp dữ liệu chuyển kho định kỳ hàng tháng theo yêu cầu kế toán."
Tạo phiếu mới	A secondary, highly visible shortcut at the bottom of the layout to quickly initiate a new transfer ticket.
3.12.2 Create Transfer Slip
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Input Transfer Details: Define the source, destination, personnel, and accounting dates for an inventory transfer operation.
●	Manage Commodity List: Manually add items row-by-row or mass-import them via Excel to specify which products are being moved.
●	Upload Supporting Documents: Attach relevant files (e.g., physical signed requests or invoices) directly to the digital slip.
●	Process the Workflow: Choose to save the slip as a draft, commit it to the system, or save and print it immediately for warehouse logistics.
Field Name
Description
Kho xuất 	The origin warehouse from which stock will be deducted.
Kho nhập	The receiving warehouse where stock will be added.
Nhân viên thực hiện	Auto-populated with the currently logged-in user (e.g., Nguyễn Văn An), but allows reassignment if the user has appropriate permissions.
Dự án / Tham chiếu	A reference code linking this transfer to a specific project or external document.
Lý do chuyển	Context or justification for the inventory movement.
Số phiếu	System auto-generated sequence
Ngày hạch toán	The date the transfer will be recorded in the financial/accounting ledger.
Ngày chứng từ	The date written on the physical transfer document (usually defaults to today).
Nhập từ Excel	Opens a dialog to upload an .xlsx file to populate the item grid instantly.
Thêm dòng	Appends a new blank row to the bottom of the item grid for manual data entry.
Đính kèm	Displays the current number of successfully uploaded files 
Hủy	Discards all inputs, prompts a confirmation warning ("Bạn có chắc muốn hủy? Dữ liệu chưa lưu sẽ bị mất"), and returns to the Transfer List.
Lưu tạm	Saves the current form data into the database with a "Draft" (Lưu nháp) status. Bypasses strict inventory availability validations until actual submission.
Lưu lại	Triggers full backend validation. If successful, creates the ticket, deducts/allocates inventory, and redirects to the Transfer List with a success message.
Lưu và In	Performs the identical function to "Lưu lại", but upon success, immediately triggers the browser's native print dialog with a formatted A4/A5 PDF template of the transfer slip.

●	Modify Supplementary Info: Directly update non-impactful fields (such as Transfer Reason, Project/Reference code, or Attachments) right on the detail interface without altering actual inventory numbers.
●	Execute Workflow Actions: Perform auxiliary actions such as printing the slip, exporting data, or saving the newly updated supplementary notes.

3.12.4 Update Transfer Slip 
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Load Existing Data: Automatically retrieve and populate the form with the current data of the drafted or pending transfer slip.
●	Modify Logistics & Documentation: Update the destination warehouse, personnel, dates, or supplementary notes as needed.
●	Adjust Commodity List: Add new items, remove existing items, or change the transfer quantities before final commitment.
●	Apply Updates: Save the modifications back as a draft, or finalize the ticket to trigger inventory movement.
Field Name
Description
Breadcrumb Menu 	Navigational tracking path: Trang chủ > Quản lý kho > Chuyển kho > Cập nhật phiếu
Form Title	Display text header: "Cập nhật phiếu chuyển kho"
Kho xuất	Business Rule: Once a transfer ticket is generated (even as a draft), the source warehouse is typically locked. This prevents breaking inventory allocation logic or "soft holds" placed on the stock when the draft was initially saved. To change the source, the user must cancel this ticket and create a new one.
Kho nhập	Allows changing the receiving branch if the items need to be rerouted before final approval.
Nhân viên thực hiện	The staff member responsible for the operation.
Dự án / Tham chiếu	Optional reference code linking to external projects.
Lý do chuyển	Justification for the transfer.
Số phiếu	The system-generated ID (e.g., CK00001) cannot be changed.
Ngày hạch toán	Can be adjusted if the financial recording period changes.
Ngày chứng từ	Data Type: Date Picker. State: Editable.
Nhập từ Excel / Thêm dòng	Allows users to append new items to the transfer slip.
Item Data Grid	Columns: STT, Mã hàng, Tên hàng hóa, ĐVT, Số lượng, Đơn giá, Thành tiền. State: Editable.
Business Rule: If the user modifies the Số lượng (Quantity), the system must re-trigger real-time validation against the available stock in the locked Kho xuất. If the new quantity exceeds available stock, highlight the cell in red and block submission.
Hủy	Discards all unsaved modifications and returns the user to the Transfer List.
Lưu tạm	Overwrites the existing draft with the new modifications but keeps the status as "Lưu tạm" (Draft).
Lưu lại	Triggers full validation. Commits the ticket, updates the status, deducts inventory, and redirects to the Transfer List.
Lưu và In 	Commits the updates to the system and immediately opens the print dialog for the physical slip.

3.13 Stocktake Management
3.13.1 View Stocktake History List
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Monitor Inventory Audits: View a comprehensive, paginated grid of all physical stocktake/inventory count tickets (phiếu kiểm kê) across the organization.
●	Filter Audit Records: Utilize multi-criteria dropdown filters (Timeframe, Status, and Location) to quickly isolate specific stocktake sessions.
●	Manage Stocktake Workflows: Initiate a new inventory count ticket or import physical count data in batches via Excel.
●	Analyze Discrepancies: Quickly scan the variance column to identify shortages or surpluses between system records and physical counts.
●	Generate Variance Reports: Access quick reporting tools at the bottom of the screen to download automated Excel summaries of inventory discrepancies.
●	
Field Name
Description
Global Sidebar Navigation	Left-hand menu containing system modules. The Kiểm kê kho (Stocktake) menu item is currently active.
Top Search Bar	Global search input for quickly locating specific stocktake ticket IDs across the system.
Tạo phiếu kiểm kê	Redirects the user to the "Create Stocktake Ticket" form. Features a primary blue color styling.
Data Grid Component	Dynamic tabular view displaying stocktake records.
Ngày kiểm kê	The exact date the inventory audit was initiated or completed
Mã phiếu	The unique system-generated identifier for the stocktake session
Kho kiểm kê	The specific warehouse facility where the physical count is taking place
Nhân viên	The name of the inventory staff or manager responsible for conducting the physical count. 
Số lượng lệch	Displays the absolute numerical difference between the system's expected stock and the physically counted stock.
Trạng thái	Indicates the current phase of the audit. Values include:
• Hoàn tất: The audit is closed, and system records have been updated to match physical counts.
• Đang kiểm: The physical counting process is actively ongoing.
• Bản nháp: Draft
Thao tác	Inline actions allow users to view detailed ticket information (Eye icon) or print the stocktake sheet (Printer icon).
3.13.2 Create Stocktake 
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Define Audit Scope: Select the specific warehouse location, assign the responsible staff member, and set the date for the physical inventory count.
●	Input Physical Counts: Build the list of items being audited (manually or via bulk Excel import) and input the actual physical quantities found on the warehouse floor.
●	Analyze Discrepancies: Automatically view the calculated variance (shortages or surpluses) between the database records and physical reality in real-time.
●	Manage the Audit Lifecycle: Save the session as a draft to continue counting later, or finalize the ticket to formally record the variances for managerial approval and inventory adjustment.

Field Name
Description
Form Title	Display text header: "Tạo phiếu kiểm kê kho"
Kho kiểm kê	Data Type: Select Dropdown. Mandatory. Description: The specific facility being audited.
Business Rule: Changing this dropdown after items have been added to the grid below should trigger a warning: "Thay đổi kho sẽ xóa toàn bộ danh sách hàng hóa đang kiểm kê. Bạn có chắc chắn?" because system stock levels are strictly tied to the selected warehouse.
Nhân viên kiểm kê	Data Type: Select Dropdown. Mandatory. Initial Data: Auto-populated with the logged-in user.
Mục đích kiểm kê	Data Type: String. Optional. Description: The reason for the count
Trạng thái	Data Type: String / Badge. Default: "Đang kiểm".
Ngày kiểm kê	Data Type: Date Picker. Mandatory. Format: DD/MM/YYYY. Default: Current date.
Ghi chú	Data Type: Textarea. Optional. Description: Additional internal notes regarding the audit conditions.
Nhập từ Excel	Data Type: Action Button. Description: Opens a dialog to upload an Excel file containing item codes and their physically counted quantities for rapid bulk entry.
Thêm dòng	Data Type: Action Button. Description: Appends a blank row for manual data entry.
Hủy	Data Type: Cancel Button. Description: Discards the current session, warns the user of data loss if the grid is populated, and returns to the Stocktake History List.
Lưu nháp	Data Type: Secondary Action Button. Description: Saves the ticket data into the database with a "Draft / Đang kiểm" status. Does not generate inventory adjustment logs. Allows the team to resume counting later.
Hoàn thành	Data Type: Primary Action Button. Description: Triggers backend validation. Changes status to "Chờ duyệt" (Pending Approval) or "Đã cân bằng" (Balanced) depending on the approval workflow configuration. Locks the “SL thực tế”  column from further edits.
3.13.3 Select Reference Document
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Select Audit Records: Choose a finalized stocktake ticket to begin the reconciliation workflow.
●	Review Variances: Inspect the discrepancies (shortages or surpluses) between system records and physical counts loaded automatically from the selected ticket.
●	Determine Resolutions: View the system-determined inventory adjustment actions (e.g., creating an export ticket for lost items, or an import ticket for surplus items).
●	Execute Balancing: Apply the adjustments to definitively sync the database inventory with the physical reality.
Field Name
Description
Form Title	Display text: "Đối soát và Cân bằng kho"
Subtitle Instruction	Display text: "Vui lòng chọn phiếu kiểm kê đã hoàn thành để thực hiện đối soát." (Please select a completed stocktake ticket to reconcile.)
Chọn phiếu kiểm kê	Data Type: Searchable Dropdown. State: Mandatory.
Business Rule: This dropdown must only load tickets that are currently in a "Chờ duyệt" (Pending Approval) or "Chờ đối soát" (Pending Reconciliation) status. Tickets that are already "Đã cân bằng" (Balanced) or "Đang kiểm" (Checking) must be strictly excluded from this list to prevent double-adjustments or adjusting incomplete counts.
Hành động xử lý	Data Type: String / Badge. State: System Auto-generated.
Critical Business Rule:
• If Variance < 0 (Shortage): Display "Tạo phiếu xuất kho bù trừ" (Create adjustment export slip).
• If Variance > 0 (Surplus): Display "Tạo phiếu nhập kho bổ sung" (Create adjustment import slip).
• If Variance = 0: The row should ideally be hidden, or display "Không cần xử lý" (No action needed).
Hủy bỏ	Data Type: Cancel Navigation Button. Description: Clears the selected dropdown data, empties the grid, and safely closes the modal without making any system changes.
Thực hiện cân bằng	Data Type: Primary Action Button. Description: Triggers the core reconciliation workflow.
Post-Conditions (Happy Path): Upon clicking, the system must perform the following transactions atomically:
1. Auto-generate the necessary "Nhập kho" (Import) and "Xuất kho" (Export) slips in the background, tagging them with a specific system reason (e.g., "Cân bằng kho tự động từ phiếu KK00214").
2. Update the actual master stock levels based on these new slips.
3. Change the status of the selected Stocktake Ticket to "Đã cân bằng" (Balanced).
4. Close the modal and display a success toast message: "Cân bằng kho thành công".
3.13.4 View Stocktake Details 
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	View Comprehensive Audit Data: Access read-only core information regarding the physical count session, including the target warehouse, responsible staff, system stock, and actual quantities.
●	Track Discrepancies: Inspect the detailed surplus or shortage for each item/component to support accountability, reconciliation, or accounting explanations.
●	Modify Supplementary Info (Hybrid Mechanism): Update non-impactful fields such as "Notes" or "Audit Purpose" directly on the interface without altering actual inventory figures.
●	Execute Auxiliary Actions: Print physical stocktake slips or export variance data to Excel/PDF for external reporting.
Field Name
Description
Breadcrumb Menu	Navigational tracking path: Trang chủ > Quản lý kho > Kiểm kê kho > Chi tiết phiếu kiểm kê
Form Title & Status Badge	Display text: "Chi tiết phiếu kiểm kê: [Mã phiếu]" (Stocktake Ticket Detail: [Ticket Code]). Accompanied by a color-coded status badge
Kho kiểm kê	Data Type: String. State: Read-only. Displays the facility being audited to prevent post-count data manipulation.
Nhân viên kiểm kê	The staff member responsible for the physical count.
Ngày kiểm kê	Data Type: Date/Time. State: Read-only. The exact timestamp when the system stock snapshot was taken.
Mục đích kiểm kê	Data Type: Text Input. State: Editable (if not yet balanced). Clarifies the reason for the audit (e.g., Monthly Audit, Suspected Loss).
Ghi chú	Data Type: Textarea. State: Editable (if not yet balanced). Records any observations or issues found during the count (e.g., damaged boxes, misplaced items).
Bảng chi tiết (Data Grid)	Detailed data grid of the audited items.
Business Rule: The entire grid is strictly Read-only. Users cannot edit quantities, add rows, or delete rows here.
Khối tổng hợp số liệu	Pinned at the bottom of the grid:
• Tổng số lượng thực tế (Total Actual Qty): Sum of all counted items.
• Tổng lệch tăng (Total Surplus): Sum of all positive variances (Displayed in Green).
• Tổng lệch giảm (Total Shortage): Absolute sum of all negative variances (Displayed in Red).
Quay lại	Data Type: Navigation Button. Description: Discards any unsaved changes in the Notes/Purpose fields and returns to the Stocktake History List.
In phiếu	Data Type: Action Button. Description: Opens the print dialog for a standardized physical stocktake slip (including signature lines for staff and managers).
Xuất Excel / PDF	Data Type: Export Button. Description: Downloads the detailed variance report for internal review.
Lưu thay đổi	Data Type: Primary Action Button. State: Default is Disabled. It becomes Active only when the user modifies the Purpose or Notes. Clicking it commits these supplementary updates to the database.
3.13.5 Update Stocktake 
 
This screen allows the Manager, Staff to:
●	Resume Audit Session: Automatically load the saved draft data of an ongoing physical inventory count.
●	Modify General Info: Update supplementary details such as the assigned staff, audit date, purpose, or internal notes.
●	Adjust Counted Items: Add new items to the audit list, remove items, or update the physically counted quantities (SL Thực tế).
●	Manage Workflow: Save the progress back as a draft to continue later, or finalize the count to submit it for managerial review and variance reconciliation.
Field Name
Description
Breadcrumb Menu	Navigational tracking path: Trang chủ > Quản lý kho > Kiểm kê kho > Cập nhật phiếu
Form Title	Display text header: "Cập nhật phiếu kiểm kê kho"
Kho kiểm kê	Data Type: String. State: Read-only / Disabled.
Critical Business Rule: Once a stocktake ticket is created, the target warehouse cannot be changed. This is because the Tồn kho HT (System Stock) snapshot is intrinsically tied to the specific facility at the exact time of creation. To change the warehouse, the user must cancel this ticket and create a new one.
Nhân viên kiểm kê	Data Type: Select Dropdown. State: Editable. Description: The staff member actively conducting or responsible for the count.
Mục đích kiểm kê	Data Type: Text Input. State: Editable. Description: The reason for the audit (e.g., Monthly Audit).
Trạng thái	Data Type: Visual Badge. State: Read-only. Displays the current workflow state (e.g., "Đang kiểm" - Checking).
Ngày kiểm kê	Data Type: Date Picker. State: Editable. Format: DD/MM/YYYY.
Ghi chú	Data Type: Textarea. State: Editable. Description: Additional observations during the count.
Hủy	Data Type: Cancel Button. Description: Discards any unsaved modifications made during this session and returns the user to the Stocktake History List.
Lưu nháp	Data Type: Secondary Action Button. Description: Overwrites the existing draft in the database with the newly inputted quantities and notes. The status remains "Đang kiểm" (Checking).
Hoàn thành	Data Type: Primary Action Button. Description: Triggers full validation. Submits the physical counts, locks the ticket from further edits.

3.14.2 Create Assembly Order
 

This screen allows the Manager / Staff to create a new assembly order by selecting finished products and the required components. The system will consume component inventory and generate the assembled product according to the defined BOM (Bill of Materials).
The user can:
●	Create a new assembly order.
●	Enter assembly order information.
●	Select finished products to be assembled.
●	Select components required for assembly.
●	Specify quantities for finished products and components.
●	Calculate assembly costs automatically.
●	Save the assembly order.
●	Save and create another assembly order.
●	Cancel the creation process.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View the generated assembly order number.
●	View the transaction date.
●	Enter notes or descriptions for the assembly transaction.
●	Add or remove finished products.
●	Add or remove assembly components.
●	Load predefined BOM information.
●	Review the total value of the assembly order before saving.

Field Name	Description

Reference	Displays reference information related to the transaction.
Total Amount	Displays the total value of the assembly order.
Finished Product Section	Displays products that will be created after assembly.
Product Code	Displays or allows selection of the finished product code.
Product Name	Displays the finished product name.
Unit	Displays the unit of measure of the finished product.
Quantity	Displays the quantity of finished products to be assembled.
Unit Cost	Displays the calculated assembly cost per product.
Amount	Displays the total value of the finished product line.

Warehouse	Displays the warehouse supplying the component inventory.

Cất	Saves the assembly order and closes the screen.
Cất và In	Saves the assembly order and prints the document.
Cất và Thêm	Saves the assembly order and opens a new blank form.
 


3.14.3 Create Disassembly Order
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to create a new disassembly order by selecting finished products to be dismantled into individual components. The system will reduce finished product inventory and return the recovered components back to inventory.

●	Enter disassembly order information.

●	Specify quantities of products to be disassembled.
●	Review the components recovered after disassembly.

●	Save the disassembly order.
●	Save and create another disassembly order.

●	View the generated disassembly order number.
●	View transaction date information.
●	Enter notes or descriptions.
●	Automatically load component information from the BOM configuration.
●	Add or remove products from the disassembly order.
●	Review recovered component quantities before saving.

Field Name	Description

Product Code	Displays or allows selection of the product code to be disassembled.
Product Name	Displays the product name.
Unit	Displays the unit of measure.

Quantity	Displays the quantity returned to inventory.
Add Row (Component)	Adds a new component row.
Delete Row (Component)	Removes the selected component row.
Hủy	Cancels the transaction and closes the screen without saving.
Cất	Saves the disassembly order and closes the screen.
Cất và In	Saves the disassembly order and prints the document.
Cất và Thêm	Saves the disassembly order and opens a new blank form.
 

3.14.4 View Assembly/Disassembly Details
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to view the detailed information of a completed or pending assembly/disassembly order. The screen provides a complete overview of the transaction, including source products, generated products/components, inventory impacts, and related warehouse documents.
The user can:
●	View assembly/disassembly order information.
●	Review transaction details.
●	View assembled products or recovered components.
●	Review quantities and inventory values.
●	View related inventory vouchers.
●	Print the transaction document.
●	Edit the transaction if the status permits.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View order number and transaction date.
●	View transaction description and reference information.
●	View linked warehouse receipt and warehouse issue vouchers.
●	Review all products and components involved in the transaction.
●	Access utility functions from the Tiện ích menu.
●	Print transaction information.
●	Return to the previous screen.

Field Name	Description
Order Number	Displays the unique assembly/disassembly order number (e.g., LRTD00001).
Date	Displays the transaction date.
Description	Displays notes or explanations related to the transaction.
Reference	Displays linked inventory documents generated by the transaction.
Total Amount	Displays the total transaction value.
Auto Generate Components	Indicates whether components were automatically generated from a BOM definition.
Product Section	Displays products selected for assembly or disassembly.
Product Code	Displays the product code.
Product Name	Displays the product name.
Unit	Displays the unit of measure.
Quantity	Displays the transaction quantity.
Unit Cost	Displays the unit cost.
Amount	Displays the total line amount.
Component / Finished Product Section	Displays generated products or recovered components.
Component Code	Displays component code or generated product code.
Component Name	Displays component or generated product name.
Unit	Displays unit of measure.
Quantity	Displays quantity generated or recovered.
Related Inventory Receipt	Displays the inventory receipt voucher generated by the transaction.
Related Inventory Issue	Displays the inventory issue voucher generated by the transaction.
Print	Prints the transaction document.
Utilities	Opens additional utility functions.
Edit	Opens the transaction in edit mode if editing is allowed.
Back	Returns to the previous screen.
Cất và In	Saves the disassembly order and prints the document.
Cất và Thêm	Saves the disassembly order and opens a new blank form.
 

3.14.5 Update Assembly/Disassembly
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to modify an existing assembly or disassembly order before the transaction is finalized. The user can update transaction information, adjust product quantities, modify component details, and recalculate inventory values.
The user can:
●	Update assembly/disassembly order information.
●	Modify transaction descriptions.
●	Change transaction dates.
●	Add, remove, or update products.
●	Add, remove, or update components.
●	Adjust assembly/disassembly quantities.
●	Save changes to the transaction.
●	Save and print the updated transaction.
●	Cancel the update process.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View the current order number.
●	View related inventory documents.
●	Automatically regenerate components from BOM definitions.
●	Review recalculated transaction values.
●	Validate inventory availability before saving.
●	Access utility functions.




Field Name	Description
Order Number	Displays the assembly/disassembly order number being updated (e.g., LRTD00001).
Date	Displays and allows modification of the transaction date.
Description	Text box used to modify transaction notes or explanations.
Reference	Displays related inventory vouchers associated with the transaction.
Total Amount	Displays the recalculated total transaction value.
Auto Generate Components	Checkbox used to automatically regenerate components based on the BOM configuration.
Product Section	Displays products selected for assembly or disassembly.
Product Code	Displays or allows modification of the product code.
Product Name	Displays the product name.
Unit	Displays the unit of measure.
Quantity	Displays and allows modification of transaction quantity.
Unit Cost	Displays the current unit cost.
Amount	Displays the total amount of each line item.
Component Section	Displays components consumed or generated by the transaction.
Component Code	Displays component code.
Component Name	Displays component name.
Unit	Displays component unit of measure.
Quantity	Displays and allows modification of component quantity.
Add Row	Adds a new product or component line.
Delete Row	Removes the selected product or component line.
Hủy	Cancels the update process and returns to the previous screen without saving changes.
Hoãn	Temporarily saves the transaction in draft status for future editing.
Cất	Saves the updated transaction.
Cất và In	Saves the transaction and prints the document.
 

3.15 Warranty Management
3.15.1 View Warranty List
 
This screen allows the User to:
●	View all warranty slips created by the store.
●	Search warranty slips using multiple search criteria.
●	Filter warranty slips by customer, product, status, technician, and warranty expiration date.
●	View detailed information of a selected warranty slip.
●	Create a new warranty slip.
●	Export warranty data for reporting purposes.
●	Print warranty information.
●	Monitor warranty processing status and assigned technicians.
Field Description
Field Name	Description
Warranty Slip No.	Data type: VARCHAR(20). Unique warranty slip identifier. Format: BH-XXXXXX. Searchable field.
Customer	Data type: NVARCHAR(100). Customer name associated with the warranty slip. Searchable field.
Product	Data type: NVARCHAR(200). Product name or product code under warranty. Searchable field.
Status	Dropdown list. Available values: All, Pending, Processing, Completed, Rejected. Default value: All.
Warranty Expiry Date	Data type: DATE. Used to filter warranty records based on expiration date.
Technician	Dropdown list containing active technicians. Default value: All Technicians.
Apply Filter	Button. Executes search and filtering based on selected criteria.
Clear Filter	Button. Resets all filter conditions to default values.
Add New	Button. Opens the Create Warranty Slip screen.
Refresh	Button. Reloads the warranty list with the latest data from the system.
Export File	Button. Exports warranty records to Excel/CSV format.
Print	Button. Prints selected warranty slip information.
More Actions	Dropdown button providing additional actions for selected records.
Select Checkbox	Checkbox used for selecting one or multiple warranty slips for bulk operations.
Warranty Slip No. (Grid Column)	Displays the warranty slip number. Clickable to open Warranty Detail screen.
Customer Name	Displays customer full name.
Phone Number	Displays customer phone number used for warranty lookup.
Product Code	Displays product identifier code.
Product Name	Displays product name covered under warranty.
Serial Number	Displays product serial number (if available).
Created Date	Data type: DATE. Date when the warranty slip was created.
Warranty Expiry Date (Grid Column)	Displays the warranty expiration date committed by the store.
Status (Grid Column)	Displays current warranty processing status using color-coded labels.
Assigned Technician	Displays technician responsible for handling the warranty request.
Records Per Page	Dropdown list for selecting the number of records displayed per page.
Pagination Control	Navigation control for moving between pages of warranty records.
Total Warranty Records	Displays the total number of warranty slips matching current filter criteria.

Low Stock Items List	Displays products that have reached or are below the predefined minimum stock level.
Warehouse	Displays the warehouse where the low-stock item is stored.
Current Stock Quantity	Displays the current available quantity of the product.
Minimum Stock Level	Displays the minimum stock threshold configured for the product.
Dashboard Period Filter	Allows users to select the reporting period displayed on the dashboard (e.g., This Month).
Refresh Dashboard	Reloads dashboard data and updates all displayed metrics.
Widget Settings	Allows users to customize dashboard widgets and display preferences.
Last Updated Time	Displays the most recent date and time when the dashboard data was refreshed.
Reload Data	Allows users to manually refresh the widget data.

3.16.7 View Inventory Summary Report
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to view summarized inventory movements within a selected period. The report provides an overview of opening balance, inventory receipts, inventory issues, and ending inventory quantities and values for each product in each warehouse.
The user can:
●	View inventory summary information by warehouse and product.
●	Monitor inventory movement during a selected period.
●	Analyze opening stock, incoming stock, outgoing stock, and ending stock.
●	Search inventory records.
●	Save report templates.
●	Load previously saved report configurations.
●	Export report data.
●	Print reports.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	Select report parameters.
●	Filter inventory data by warehouse, product, category, or reporting period.
●	View quantity and value information separately.
●	Navigate through report pages.
●	Export report results to Excel or PDF.
●	Print the report directly.
Fields Description
Field Name	Description
Danh sách báo cáo	Link used to navigate back to the report catalog screen.
Danh sách báo cáo đã lưu	Displays previously saved report configurations.
Lưu báo cáo	Button used to save the current report configuration for future use.
Chọn tham số	Button used to open the report parameter selection screen.
Search	Text box used to search products or warehouses within the report.
Warehouse Name	Displays the warehouse where inventory transactions occurred.
Product Code	Displays the unique product code.
Product Name	Displays the product name.
Unit	Displays the unit of measure of the product.
Opening Quantity	Displays inventory quantity at the beginning of the reporting period.
Opening Value	Displays inventory value at the beginning of the reporting period.
Receipt Quantity	Displays total quantity received during the reporting period.
Receipt Value	Displays total receipt value during the reporting period.
Issue Quantity	Displays total quantity issued during the reporting period.
Issue Value	Displays total issue value during the reporting period.
Ending Quantity	Displays inventory quantity at the end of the reporting period.
Ending Value	Displays inventory value at the end of the reporting period.
Total Row	Displays aggregated totals for all displayed records.
Export Excel	Exports the report to an Excel file.
Export PDF	Exports the report to a PDF file.
Print	Prints the current report.
Pagination	Allows navigation between report pages.

 

3.16.8 View Business Process Flow
 
This screen allows the Manager / Staff to view the overall warehouse business process flow and quickly navigate to related operational functions and reports. The screen provides a visual representation of inventory workflows, helping users understand the relationships between warehouse activities.
The user can:
●	View warehouse business process diagrams.
●	Understand the relationship between inventory operations.
●	Navigate directly to warehouse functions.
●	Access frequently used reports.
●	View operational workflows from inventory receipt to inventory issue.
●	Access master data management functions.
On the screen, s/he can also:
●	View warehouse workflow diagrams.
●	Access inventory-related modules through graphical shortcuts.
●	View available reports from the report panel.
●	Navigate to warehouse configuration screens.
●	Access utility and system configuration functions.

4. Non-Functional Requirements
4.1 External Interfaces
4.1.1 User Interfaces
●	The system shall provide a responsive web interface that can be used on desktop computers, laptops, and mobile browsers.
●	The interface shall support at least common browsers such as Chrome, Edge, and Firefox in their current versions.
●	The main business screens shall include: login, dashboard, stock receipt, stock issue, stock transfer, POS sales, product management, customer management, supplier management, warranty management, and reporting.
●	Frequent business operations shall be optimized for fast data entry, with minimal steps and reduced unnecessary page transitions.
●	The interface shall clearly display document status, error warnings, action confirmations, and processing results.
4.1.2 Hardware Interfaces
●	The system shall support barcode scanners or QR code scanners for product lookup and fast transaction processing.
●	The system shall be compatible with receipt printers and document printers used at the sales counter and in the warehouse.
●	If deployed at the POS counter, the system shall support touchscreen operation.
●	If other peripheral devices such as handheld scanners or label printers are used, the system shall allow connection configuration at the application or browser level.
4.1.3 Software Interfaces
●	The system shall store and retrieve data using a relational database, ensuring consistency across all modules.
●	The system shall provide internal APIs for business modules such as inventory, sales, warranty, customers, and reporting.
●	The system shall support data export in common formats such as Excel or CSV for reconciliation and internal reporting.
●	If integration with external systems is required in the future, the software interface shall support extension through REST API.
●	Data exchange messages shall have a clear structure, including error codes and complete response statuses.
4.1.4 Communication Interfaces
●	The system shall operate over standard TCP/IP network connections.
●	Data exchanged between the browser and the server shall use HTTPS for security.
●	The system shall support email or OTP notifications if customer authentication or transaction confirmation is required.
●	Synchronous transactions between the client and server shall provide clear success/failure responses.
●	If data synchronization or import/export functions are used, the system shall define the data format, encoding, and error-handling mechanism.
4.2 Quality Attributes
4.2.1 Usability 
●	New users shall be able to perform basic operations after a short training period without requiring advanced instruction.
●	Common actions such as creating stock receipts, stock issues, searching products, scanning codes, and printing documents shall have short and intuitive workflows.
●	The system shall maintain consistent naming for data fields, buttons, and document statuses across the entire interface.
●	Error messages shall be easy to understand and shall clearly indicate the cause and the required corrective action.
●	The interface shall follow familiar enterprise application design principles, prioritizing learnability and ease of use.
4.2.2 Performance
●	Response time for normal catalog lookup operations shall be near instantaneous to ensure a fast data-entry experience.
●	Creating or saving business documents shall respond quickly and shall not interrupt the workflow.
●	The system shall support multiple concurrent users without significant degradation in processing speed.
●	Searches by product code, product name, serial number, or document number shall return results quickly even as the database grows.
●	Performance shall be evaluated using clear indicators such as response time, throughput, and data-handling capacity.
4.2.3 Reliability
●	The system shall ensure that transaction data is not lost after a document has been successfully saved.
●	All posted documents shall be traceable through their change history.
●	In case of system errors or network interruptions, the data currently being processed shall be preserved as much as possible.
●	The system shall minimize discrepancies between physical stock and system stock.
●	High-risk operations shall require confirmation before execution.
4.2.4 Security
●	The system shall authenticate users before allowing access to business functions.
●	Authorization shall be based on roles or functions, restricting users to their permitted scope.
●	Sensitive data such as customer information, phone numbers, email addresses, and transaction history shall be protected.
●	User sessions shall have appropriate expiration mechanisms to reduce the risk of unauthorized access.
●	Important actions such as deleting, canceling, or editing documents shall be fully logged.
4.2.5 Maintainability
●	The system shall be designed using a modular architecture to support maintenance, expansion, and defect fixing.
●	Source code shall be organized clearly by business module.
●	Frequently changing configuration data should be separated from business logic to simplify updates.
●	The system shall have a stable data structure and API design to support future upgrades.
●	Functional and workflow documentation shall be complete enough to support long-term maintenance.
4.2.6 Scalability
●	The system shall be able to scale as the number of documents, products, and users increases.
●	The design shall allow new warehouses, branches, or sales points to be added without major architectural changes.
●	Historical data shall be stored in a way that keeps lookup efficient as the system grows.
●	The application architecture shall support horizontal scaling or load optimization when necessary.
●	Reporting and search modules shall be able to handle data growth over time.
4.2.7 Compatibility
●	The system shall be compatible with modern browser environments.
●	If peripheral devices such as printers or barcode scanners are used, the system shall be compatible with widely available devices on the market.
●	Exported data shall use common formats so it can be easily used with office software.
●	The system shall operate stably in a local network or on a standard internet connection.
4.2.8 Portability
●	The system shall be deployable on common server environments.
●	The web interface shall run correctly across multiple screen resolutions.
●	Migration to a new deployment environment shall not depend heavily on proprietary platforms.
●	Data and configuration shall be backed up and restored when needed.
4.2.9 Auditability
●	All stock receipt, stock issue, stock transfer, sales, and warranty transactions shall record the performer, execution time, and change details.
●	The system shall store document processing history for checking and reconciliation.
●	Actions such as canceling, editing, or reversing documents shall be clearly recorded.
●	Inventory checks and reconciliation reports shall contain enough data to trace the source of discrepancies.
4.2.10 Availability
●	The system shall be available during business hours with minimal interruption.
●	If maintenance is required, the system should notify users in advance and minimize disruption to ongoing transactions.
●	Core functions such as lookup, document creation, and stock viewing shall always be prioritized for availability.
●	Backup mechanisms shall be in place to reduce the risk of service loss.
5. Requirement Appendix
5.1 Business Rules  
ID	Rule Definition
I. System & Access Management
BR-01	An inactive or unapproved account cannot log into the system.
BR-02	The OTP (One-Time Password) generated for password recovery is valid for a maximum of 5 minutes.
BR-03	If a user enters incorrect login credentials 5 consecutive times, the system will temporarily lock the account for 30 minutes.
BR-04	Only users with the SUPER_ADMIN role have the permission to create, lock, or configure access rights for other user accounts.
BR-05	Dynamic module-level permissions (View/Add/Edit/Delete specific screens) are only applicable to accounts assigned to the STAFF group.
BR-06	All data modification actions (Create/Update/Delete) and Login/Logout events must be automatically recorded in the System Audit Log.
BR-07	The Audit Log operates on an append-only principle. No user, including SUPER_ADMIN, has the right to modify or delete the log history.
BR-08	User passwords must be irreversibly hashed before being stored in the database. Passwords cannot be stored or viewed in plain text.
II. Master Data (Product, Category, Supplier, Customer)
BR-09	Product Code, Category Code, and Partner (Supplier/Customer) Code must be unique identifiers across the entire system.
BR-10	When created, a product must be assigned to exactly one active Category and one active Unit of Measurement.
BR-11	A Product/Customer/Supplier cannot be permanently deleted if there is an existing transaction history. Their status can only be changed to "Inactive".
BR-12	The Default Selling Price and Purchase Price of a product must never be a negative value.
BR-13	Upon creation, a product can be configured for Serial Tracking or Quantity Tracking. This configuration cannot be changed once the first import transaction occurs.
BR-14	Core electronic components (e.g., CPU, GPU, Mainboard, Laptop) must have the Serial Tracking flag enabled.
III. Warehouse & Inventory Management
BR-15	Warehouse Code must be unique. Two warehouses cannot share the same code.
BR-16	A warehouse cannot be deleted if the actual Quantity On Hand for any item within that warehouse is greater than 0.
BR-17	A warehouse cannot be deleted if there are pending Import, Export, or Transfer slips associated with it.
BR-18	A Low Stock Alert is automatically triggered when a product's inventory quantity reaches or falls below the configured Minimum Stock level.
IV. Import Management
BR-19	Every Import Slip must contain at least one line item with a quantity and unit price greater than 0.
BR-20	For serial-tracked products, an Import Slip cannot be completed unless the number of scanned physical serials exactly matches the declared import quantity.
BR-21	The system automatically rejects the import of a serial number if that exact serial currently exists with an "In Stock" status in the system.
BR-22	An Import Slip in "Draft" status allows modification of all information. Once transitioned to "Completed" status, it becomes immutable.
BR-23	Immediately upon completion of an Import Slip, the system adds the quantities to On-Hand Inventory and records the transaction for cost calculation.
V. Export Management
BR-24	The system blocks the creation of an Export Slip if the requested quantity exceeds the actual available Quantity On Hand in the source warehouse.
BR-25	Inventory quantities must never hold negative values (Negative inventory is strictly prohibited).
BR-26	When exporting a serial-tracked product, the exact physical serial numbers being removed must be scanned and identified.
BR-27	The WMS must validate exported serials: the serial must currently reside in the specific warehouse designated by the export slip. An error is thrown if the location mismatches.
BR-28	When an Export Slip reaches "Completed" status, inventory quantity is deducted, and the corresponding Serials change status to "Sold".
BR-29	The warranty period for serial-tracked products begins tracking (Warranty Start Date) from the date the Export Slip is recorded as Completed.
VI. Stock Transfer & Stocktake
BR-30	An internal Transfer Slip must have completely distinct Source and Destination Warehouses.
BR-31	While a physical warehouse is actively associated with an open Stocktake slip, no import or export transactions can be initiated or completed for that warehouse.
BR-32	A Stocktake slip must record both the System Quantity and the Actual Quantity counted at the time of inspection.
BR-33	Upon closing a Stocktake slip, if discrepancies exist, the system automatically generates an Inventory Adjustment Import (if Actual > System) or Inventory Adjustment Export (if Actual < System).
VII. BOM - Assembly & Disassembly
BR-34	An Assembly Order is only approved if the warehouse contains sufficient quantities of all component parts defined in the Bill of Materials (BOM).
BR-35	Upon completion of an Assembly Order, the system automatically generates an Export slip for the component parts and an Import slip for the Finished Good.
BR-36	When assembling a computer (PC) containing serial-tracked components, the system creates a Parent-Child relationship, locking the component serials to the finished case/system serial.
BR-37	A Disassembly Order reverses the process: it automatically deducts the finished good's inventory and adds back the inventory quantities of the separated components.
VIII. Warranty & Repair Management
BR-38	A Repair Ticket can only be successfully created for valid products: the Serial must exist in the system and be within its active Warranty Period.
BR-39	In any instance where the original warranty seal is found broken (Void Seal), the system requires the staff to change the status to "Warranty Denied" due to user-induced damage.
BR-40	When processing a warranty repair that requires part replacement, the spare parts issued are deducted from inventory as usual, but the Unit Price charged to the customer can be adjusted to 0 VND.
BR-41	Under the "100% Replacement" policy, the newly issued replacement device inherits the exact remaining warranty days of the defective device; it does NOT restart a new warranty cycle.
BR-42	If a customer requests the replacement of components that do not match the original BOM configuration standard, the system denies free warranty coverage for those third-party components.
